1	# Field App
2	
3	React Native / Expo app for gate-entry barcode scanning, product mapping, and
4	offline-first delivery receipts. Phase 1 of the Office Ops field-capture stack
5	(see `../2026-05-22_Field_App_Architecture_Analysis.md` and
6	`../PWA_vs_Native_Decision.md` for context).
7	
8	> This folder is **independent** of the rest of the umbrella repo. It has its
9	> own `package.json`, no workspace links, and is intended to be lifted into its
10	> own GitHub repo when ready. It lives here only for shared context during
11	> early development.
12	
13	## What it does (Phase 1)
14	
15	- Scan barcodes (EAN/UPC/Code128/QR) with the device camera.
16	- If the barcode is unknown, register the product (name, category, unit).
17	- If known, show the matching open order line and capture received quantity.
18	- Everything is written to a local SQLite **outbox**. A background sync worker
19	  pushes the outbox to the ingest server when online.
20	- Big buttons, haptics, status badge — designed for low-literacy operators on
21	  cheap Androids.
22	
23	Not in Phase 1: SSID/GPS pre-flight, BLE, photos, voice prompts, push, iOS
24	parity. Those are Phase 2/3 in the architecture doc.
25	
26	## Stack
27	
28	- Expo SDK 51 + React Native 0.74 + TypeScript
29	- `expo-camera` (built-in barcode scanning)
30	- `expo-sqlite` (local persistence)
31	- `@react-navigation/native-stack`
32	- `expo-haptics`, `expo-network`
33	
34	## Prerequisites
35	
36	- Node 18+ (`node -v`)
37	- A package manager (`npm`, `pnpm`, or `yarn`)
38	- An Android device with the **Expo Go** app, *or* Android Studio for a dev
39	  build, *or* a physical iOS device with Expo Go (iOS is best-effort for now)
40	
41	> Camera barcode scanning works in Expo Go on SDK 51. Once you add native
42	> modules that Expo Go doesn't ship (ML Kit, BLE, Wi-Fi), you'll switch to
43	> `expo-dev-client` and a custom dev build.
44	
45	## Install
46	
47	```bash
48	cd field-app
49	npm install        # or: pnpm install / yarn
50	```
51	
52	## Configure the backend URL
53	
54	The app posts receipts to `${EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL}/v1/capture/...`. Set it
55	before starting Metro:
56	
57	```bash
58	# Android emulator → host machine
59	export EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL="http://10.0.2.2:8080"
60	
61	# Physical device on the same Wi-Fi as your laptop
62	export EXPO_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL="http://192.168.X.Y:8080"
63	
64	export EXPO_PUBLIC_SITE_ID="site-dev"
65	```
66	
67	If no backend is running yet, the app still works fully offline — items
68	accumulate in the outbox and you can inspect them from the **Sync queue**
69	screen.
70	
71	## Run
72	
73	```bash
74	npm start          # opens Metro / Expo dev tools
75	```
76	
77	Then:
78	- Press **a** to launch on a connected Android device/emulator, **or**
79	- Scan the QR code with **Expo Go** on your phone.
80	
81	For a custom dev build (needed once you add native modules beyond Expo Go's
82	set):
83	
84	```bash
85	npm run prebuild           # generates ios/ and android/ folders
86	npm run android            # build + install dev client on connected device
87	```
88	
89	## Type-check
90	
91	```bash
92	npm run lint               # tsc --noEmit
93	```
94	
95	## Testing the golden path manually
96	
97	1. Launch the app — you should see **Field App** home with a green
98	   "✓ All synced" badge.
99	2. Tap **Scan Item**, grant camera permission.
100	3. Point at any barcode (a product in your kitchen works). The app should
101	   navigate to **New product** because the barcode is unknown.
102	4. Type a name, pick category + unit, tap **Save & continue**.
103	5. You land on **Receive item**. Use ± / quick buttons to set qty. Tap
104	   **Confirm received**.
105	6. Back on Home, the badge should say "1 waiting to send" (or 0 if you have a
106	   backend running and reachable).
107	7. Tap **Sync queue** to see the outbox rows and their status. Pull to refresh
108	   triggers a sync attempt.
109	8. Scan the same barcode again → this time it skips registration and goes
110	   straight to the receiving screen with the product pre-filled.
111	
112	## Server contract (what the outbox posts)
113	
114	- `POST /v1/capture/receipt` — body:
115	  ```json
116	  {
117	    "id": "rcp_…",
118	    "order_id": null,
119	    "order_item_id": null,
120	    "barcode": "8901234567890",
121	    "product_name": "Tata Salt 1kg",
122	    "qty": 3,
123	    "scanned_at": 1716640000000
124	  }
125	  ```
126	- `POST /v1/capture/product` — body:
127	  ```json
128	  { "barcode": "8901234567890", "name": "Tata Salt 1kg", "category": "Grocery", "unit": "Piece" }
129	  ```
130	- Headers on every request: `X-Device-Id`, `X-Site-Id`.
131	
132	The server is expected to be idempotent on the `id` field for receipts so
133	retries are safe.
134	
135	## Project layout
136	
137	```
138	field-app/
139	  App.tsx                       # boots DB + sync, mounts navigator
140	  index.ts                      # Expo entry
141	  app.json                      # Expo config (permissions, package id)
142	  src/
143	    config.ts                   # API URL, site id, sync interval
144	    db/                         # SQLite schema + CRUD
145	    sync/                       # outbox flusher + HTTP client
146	    screens/                    # Home / Scanner / Receiving / Register / Outbox / Orders
147	    components/                 # BigButton, QueueBadge
148	    navigation/                 # stack + route types
149	    theme/                      # colors, spacing, type scale
150	    utils/                      # haptics, deviceId
151	```
152	
153	## When to graduate from Expo Go
154	
155	Move to a custom dev build (`expo-dev-client` + `npm run android`) when you
156	add any of:
157	- ML Kit barcode scanner (faster than `expo-camera` on cheap devices)
158	- BLE (`react-native-ble-plx`)
159	- Wi-Fi SSID read (`react-native-wifi-reborn`)
160	- WorkManager-backed sync
161	- `react-native-vision-camera`
162	
163	That's the Phase 2 step described in the architecture doc.
164	
```

1	import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
2	import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
3	import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
4	import { GestureHandlerRootView } from 'react-native-gesture-handler';
5	import { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
6	import { palette, Text } from './src/design';
7	import { getDb } from './src/db/database';
8	import { RootNavigator } from './src/navigation/RootNavigator';
9	import { startSync, stopSync } from './src/sync/syncService';
10	
11	export default function App() {
12	  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
13	  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
14	
15	  useEffect(() => {
16	    (async () => {
17	      try {
18	        await getDb();
19	        startSync();
20	        setReady(true);
21	      } catch (e) {
22	        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));
23	      }
24	    })();
25	    return () => stopSync();
26	  }, []);
27	
28	  if (error) {
29	    return (
30	      <View style={styles.center}>
31	        <Text variant="headlineSmall" color={palette.error}>
32	          Startup failed
33	        </Text>
34	        <Text variant="bodyLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: 8, textAlign: 'center' }}>
35	          {error}
36	        </Text>
37	      </View>
38	    );
39	  }
40	
41	  if (!ready) {
42	    return (
43	      <View style={styles.center}>
44	        <ActivityIndicator color={palette.primary} size="large" />
45	      </View>
46	    );
47	  }
48	
49	  return (
50	    <GestureHandlerRootView style={{ flex: 1 }}>
51	      <SafeAreaProvider>
52	        <StatusBar style="dark" />
53	        <RootNavigator />
54	      </SafeAreaProvider>
55	    </GestureHandlerRootView>
56	  );
57	}
58	
59	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
60	  center: {
61	    flex: 1,
62	    alignItems: 'center',
63	    justifyContent: 'center',
64	    backgroundColor: palette.background,
65	    padding: 24,
66	  },
67	});
68

---README---
# CODING AGENTS: READ THIS FIRST

This is a **handoff bundle** from Claude Design (claude.ai/design).

A user mocked up designs in HTML/CSS/JS using an AI design tool, then exported this bundle so a coding agent can implement the designs for real.

## What you should do — IMPORTANT

**Read the chat transcripts first.** There are 1 chat transcript(s) in `office-operations/chats/`. The transcripts show the full back-and-forth between the user and the design assistant — they tell you **what the user actually wants** and **where they landed** after iterating. Don't skip them. The final HTML files are the output, but the chat is where the intent lives.

**Read `office-operations/project/Office Operations.html` in full.** The user had this file open when they triggered the handoff, so it's almost certainly the primary design they want built. Read it top to bottom — don't skim. Then **follow its imports**: open every file it pulls in (shared components, CSS, scripts) so you understand how the pieces fit together before you start implementing.

**If anything is ambiguous, ask the user to confirm before you start implementing.** It's much cheaper to clarify scope up front than to build the wrong thing.

## About the design files

The design medium is **HTML/CSS/JS** — these are prototypes, not production code. Your job is to **recreate them pixel-perfectly** in whatever technology makes sense for the target codebase (React, Vue, native, whatever fits). Match the visual output; don't copy the prototype's internal structure unless it happens to fit.

**Don't render these files in a browser or take screenshots unless the user asks you to.** Everything you need — dimensions, colors, layout rules — is spelled out in the source. Read the HTML and CSS directly; a screenshot won't tell you anything they don't.

## Bundle contents

- `office-operations/README.md` — this file
- `office-operations/chats/` — conversation transcripts (read these!)
- `office-operations/project/` — the `Office Operations` project files (HTML prototypes, assets, components)

1	# Office Inventory App
2	
3	_Started 2026-05-26 05:58 UTC_
4	
5	---
6	
7	## User
8	
9	An app to handle office operations, for now we are focusing on inventory management. It should be intuitive with good UI and UX, because a lot of non tech and less literate people will use it.
10	I need complete design of the app.
11	
12	## Assistant
13	
14	_[tool: read_file]_
15	
16	_[tool: read_file]_
17	
18	_[tool: read_file]_

22	_[tool: questions_v2]_
23	
24	Before I design this, I want to make sure I understand the scope. Your docs give me strong direction (gate-guard flow, low-literacy users, barcode mapping as the data flywheel, offline-first), but a few choices will shape the whole design.
25	
26	## User
27	
28	Questions answered:
29	- scope: Field app (Android, guard at the gate) — scan, register, count
30	- primary_user: Rotating staff (anyone might use it on any given day)
31	- languages: All three — icon-first, with English + Hindi labels and voice
32	- core_flows: All of the above
33	- admin_flows: Bill ingestion — upload/forward bill photo, AI extracts line items
34	- context_setting: General office (stationery, cleaning, pantry, IT consumables)
35	- variations: Mix — give me both safe and bold options side-by-side
36	- visual_direction: 1
37	- variation_axes: Overall visual style (color, type, density)
38	- fidelity: Hi-fi clickable prototype on phone frame (single file, full app)
39	- tweak_priorities: Swap between variation A/B of key screens
40

42

136	Built — a single-file hi-fi prototype on an Android frame with the full field-app flow.
137	
138	**What's in it**
139	- **Home** with tile launcher, offline/queue status, Today snapshot
140	- **Receiving**: orders grouped by arrived / awaited / done → delivery line items → **scanner** (tap viewfinder to demo a detection) → **confirm count** (+1/+5/+10/=expected with mismatch flag) → **register unknown barcode** (photo → name → category → unit) → **proof photo** → done summary
141	- **Stock** (search + category chips, status colors)
142	- **Issue/consume** (scan or search, qty + reason)
143	- **Alerts** (low/out with inline reorder)
144	- **Sync queue** screen
145	- Voice-prompt button on every screen header
146	
147	**Tweaks panel** (toolbar toggle):
148	- **Steady ↔ Bold** — same structure, two visual languages
149	- **EN / हिं / Icons** — labels swap live
150	- Online toggle + Jump-to-screen shortcuts
151	
152	**Caveats / next steps**
153	- Camera + barcode are simulated (HTML doesn't have ML Kit) — tap the viewfinder to advance
154	- Voice prompts have buttons but no audio recordings yet
155	- I designed Hindi strings myself; have someone fluent review before shipping
156	- One item (Floor cleaner) is rigged to demo the mismatch state; one barcode (`8902080000034`) is unknown to demo register flow
157	
158	Want me to expand on the admin/dashboard side (bill ingestion → AI-extracted orders) next, or push deeper on any single screen?
159	
160

> AGENT

I've read the design bundle (README, chat transcript, `tokens.jsx`, and the HTML shell) and inventoried your existing `field-app/src` (Home, Scanner, Receiving, Register, Outbox, Orders + a `design/` token system already in place).

Before I write code, here's the scope I'd recommend — please confirm or redirect:

**What the design adds beyond what you have today**
1. Receiving flow expansion — orders grouped by *arrived / awaited / done*, line items, **+1/+5/+10/=expected** quick buttons, **mismatch flag** flow, **proof photo**, done summary.
2. New screens — **Stock** (search + category chips, status colors), **Issue/consume** (scan or search, qty + reason), **Alerts** (low/out + inline reorder).
3. **i18n** (EN / हिं / Icons-only mode) and a **voice-prompt** button on each header.
4. Two visual variations (Steady = restrained, Bold = chunky/lime). The chat says they share identical structure.

**What I'd propose, given your constraints (org primary `#92288E`, future super-app w/ RBAC)**

- **Theme system**: refactor `src/design/tokens.ts` into a `ThemeProvider` with a `theme` object (palette, type, radius, shadow, density). Ship two themes — `steadyPurple` (the design's "Steady" recipe but with `#92288E` as `primary`, plus a derived `primarySoft` tint) and the design's `bold` variant — and let the screen tree consume via `useTheme()`. This keeps the design's two-variation system but defaults to your brand.
- **Super-app scaffolding**: introduce `src/modules/<moduleId>/` (start with `inventory/` and move existing screens there). Add a tiny `src/rbac/` with a `Module` registry (`id`, `title`, `icon`, `requiredPermissions`) and a `useCan(permission)` hook stubbed to allow-all for now. Home becomes a module launcher that filters by `useCan`. Attendance/Daily Tasks/Maintenance are added later by dropping in a new `modules/<id>/`.
- **i18n**: a `src/i18n/strings.ts` + `useT()` hook lifted from `tokens.jsx`'s `STR` table; language stored in AsyncStorage; icons-only mode just hides labels in icon tiles.
- **Skip** for now: the Tweaks panel (dev-only), voice prompts wired to actual audio (button stays as UI placeholder), and the literal "Bold" variant beyond having the theme slot for it.

**Implementation order** (one PR-sized chunk):
1. Theme provider + brand palette + i18n hook + module registry + RBAC stub.
2. Migrate existing screens into `modules/inventory/`, switch them to `useTheme`/`useT`.
3. New screens: Stock, Issue, Alerts, plus the receiving-flow extensions (mismatch, proof photo, summary).
4. Home → module launcher with status row (online/offline/queued).

Roughly 25–35 files touched/created. Want me to proceed with all of it, or would you rather I land step 1+2 first (foundation + migration with no visible regressions), then do the new screens in a follow-up?

land step 1+2 first (foundation +

1	import React from 'react';
2	import { StyleProp, Text as RNText, TextProps, TextStyle } from 'react-native';
3	import { palette, type } from './tokens';
4	
5	type Variant = keyof typeof type;
6	
7	type Props = TextProps & {
8	  variant?: Variant;
9	  color?: string;
10	  style?: StyleProp<TextStyle>;
11	  children: React.ReactNode;
12	};
13	
14	export function Text({ variant = 'bodyLarge', color = palette.onSurface, style, children, ...rest }: Props) {
15	  return (
16	    <RNText {...rest} style={[type[variant], { color }, style]}>
17	      {children}
18	    </RNText>
19	  );
20	}
21

81	const SIZES: Record<ButtonSize, { h: number; px: number; gap: number; variant: 'labelLarge' | 'titleMedium' | 'titleLarge' | 'headlineSmall' }> = {
82	  sm: { h: 40, px: spacing.lg, gap: spacing.sm, variant: 'labelLarge' },
83	  md: { h: 52, px: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.sm, variant: 'titleMedium' },
84	  lg: { h: 64, px: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.md, variant: 'titleLarge' },
85	  xl: { h: 88, px: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.md, variant: 'headlineSmall' },
86	};
87	
88	export function Button({
89	  label,
90	  onPress,
91	  variant = 'filled',
92	  size = 'md',
93	  leadingIcon,
94	  trailingIcon,
95	  disabled,
96	  loading,
97	  fullWidth,
98	  style,
99	  testID,
100	}: Props) {
101	  const scale = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;
102	  const t = variantTokens(variant, !!disabled || !!loading);
103	  const s = SIZES[size];
104	
105	  const animateTo = (v: number) =>
106	    Animated.timing(scale, {
107	      toValue: v,
108	      duration: motion.fast,
109	      useNativeDriver: true,
110	    }).start();
111	
112	  return (
113	    <Animated.View style={[fullWidth && { alignSelf: 'stretch' }, { transform: [{ scale }] }, style]}>
114	      <Pressable
115	        testID={testID}
116	        disabled={disabled || loading}
117	        onPressIn={() => animateTo(0.97)}
118	        onPressOut={() => animateTo(1)}
119	        onPress={() => {
120	          haptic.tap();
121	          onPress();
122	        }}
123	        android_ripple={{ color: t.pressedBg, borderless: false }}
124	        style={({ pressed }) => [
125	          styles.base,
126	          {
127	            minHeight: s.h,
128	            paddingHorizontal: s.px,
129	            backgroundColor: pressed ? t.pressedBg : t.bg,
130	            borderColor: t.border,
131	            borderWidth: variant === 'outlined' ? 1.5 : 0,
132	            opacity: disabled ? 0.6 : 1,
133	          },
134	        ]}
135	      >
136	        <View style={[styles.row, { gap: s.gap }]}>
137	          {leadingIcon ? <View>{leadingIcon}</View> : null}
138	          <Text variant={s.variant} color={t.fg}>
139	            {loading ? 'Working…' : label}
140	          </Text>
141	          {trailingIcon ? <View>{trailingIcon}</View> : null}
142	        </View>
143	      </Pressable>
144	    </Animated.View>
145	  );
146	}
147	
148	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
149	  base: {
150	    borderRadius: radius.pill,
151	    alignItems: 'center',
152	    justifyContent: 'center',
153	    overflow: 'hidden',
154	  },
155	  row: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },
156	});
157	
```

20	  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
21	  children?: React.ReactNode;
22	};
23	
24	export function Surface({

31	}: Props) {

14	  const borderColor = error
15	    ? palette.error
16	    : focused
17	    ? palette.primary
18	    : palette.outlineVariant;
19	  return (
20	    <View>
21	      <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.xs }}>
22	        {label}
23	      </Text>
24	      <TextInput
25	        {...rest}
26	        onFocus={(e) => {
27	          setFocused(true);
28	          onFocus?.(e);
29	        }}
30	        onBlur={(e) => {
31	          setFocused(false);
32	          onBlur?.(e);
33	        }}
34	        placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
35	        style={[
36	          styles.input,
37	          typo.bodyLarge,
38	          { borderColor, color: palette.onSurface, borderWidth: focused ? 2 : 1.5 },
39	          style,
40	        ]}
41	      />
42	      {(error || helper) && (
43	        <Text
44	          variant="labelMedium"
45	          color={error ? palette.error : palette.onSurfaceVariant}
46	          style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}
47	        >
48	          {error ?? helper}
49	        </Text>
50	      )}
51	    </View>
52	  );
53	}
54

57	    backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,
58	    borderRadius: radius.md,

1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
3	import { StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
4	import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
5	import { QueueBadge } from '../components/QueueBadge';

10	
11	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Home'>;
12	
13	export function HomeScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
14	  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ products: 0, orders: 0 });
15	
16	  useEffect(() => {
17	    const load = async () => {
18	      const [p, o] = await Promise.all([listProducts(1000), listOpenOrders()]);
19	      setStats({ products: p.length, orders: o.length });
20	    };
21	    load();
22	    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', load);
23	    return unsub;
24	  }, [navigation]);
25	
26	  return (
27	    <SafeAreaView edges={['top']} style={styles.safe}>
28	      <View style={styles.header}>
29	        <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
30	          GATE ENTRY
31	        </Text>
32	        <Text variant="displayMedium" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
33	          Field
34	        </Text>
35	        <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
36	          <QueueBadge />
37	        </View>
38	      </View>
39	
40	      <View style={styles.primary}>
41	        <Button
42	          label="Scan item"
43	          leadingIcon={<Text variant="headlineSmall" color={palette.onPrimary}>⌖</Text>}
44	          size="xl"
45	          fullWidth
46	          onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Scanner')}
47	        />
48	        <Text
49	          variant="bodyMedium"
50	          color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
51	          style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.md }}
52	        >
53	          Point camera at a barcode. We'll do the rest.
54	        </Text>
55	      </View>
56	
57	      <View style={styles.tiles}>
58	        <Card
59	          tone="filled"
60	          onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Orders')}
61	          style={styles.tile}
62	        >
63	          <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
64	            DELIVERIES
65	          </Text>
66	          <Text variant="displayMedium" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
67	            {stats.orders}
68	          </Text>
69	          <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
70	            expected today
71	          </Text>
72	        </Card>
73	        <Card
74	          tone="filled"
75	          onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Outbox')}
76	          style={styles.tile}
77	        >

79	            CATALOG
80	          </Text>

82	            {stats.products}
83	          </Text>

85	            products mapped
86	          </Text>
87	        </Card>
88	      </View>

90	  );

15	  useCameraPermission,
16	  useCodeScanner,
17	} from 'react-native-vision-camera';
18	import { Button, IconButton, palette, spacing, Text } from '../design';
19	import { findProduct } from '../db/products';
20	import { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';
21	import { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';
22	
23	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Scanner'>;
24	
25	// Vision-camera / ML Kit format names. Order doesn't affect decoding —
26	// every frame is tried against every requested format. Listed broadly so
27	// dense GS1-128 SSCC labels, EAN/UPC products, QR, DataMatrix all work.
28	const CODE_TYPES = [
29	  'code-128',
30	  'code-39',
31	  'code-93',
32	  'codabar',
33	  'ean-13',
34	  'ean-8',
35	  'itf',
36	  'upc-a',
37	  'upc-e',
38	  'qr',
39	  'data-matrix',
40	  'pdf-417',
41	  'aztec',
42	] as const;
43	
44	export function ScannerScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
45	  const { hasPermission, requestPermission } = useCameraPermission();
46	  const device = useCameraDevice('back');
47	  const [torch, setTorch] = useState(false);
48	  const lockedRef = useRef(false);
49	  const flashOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
50	  const linePos = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
51	
52	  useEffect(() => {
53	    if (!hasPermission) requestPermission();
54	  }, [hasPermission, requestPermission]);
55	
56	  useEffect(() => {
57	    const anim = Animated.loop(
58	      Animated.sequence([
59	        Animated.timing(linePos, {
60	          toValue: 1,
61	          duration: 1600,
62	          easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.ease),
63	          useNativeDriver: true,
64	        }),
65	        Animated.timing(linePos, {
66	          toValue: 0,
67	          duration: 1600,
68	          easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.ease),
69	          useNativeDriver: true,
70	        }),
71	      ])
72	    );
73	    anim.start();
74	    return () => anim.stop();
75	  }, [linePos]);
76	
77	  const flash = useCallback(() => {
78	    flashOpacity.setValue(0.6);
79	    Animated.timing(flashOpacity, {
80	      toValue: 0,
81	      duration: 240,
82	      useNativeDriver: true,
83	    }).start();
84	  }, [flashOpacity]);
85	
86	  const onCodeScanned = useCallback(
87	    (codes: Code[]) => {
88	      if (lockedRef.current) return;
89	      const first = codes.find((c) => c.value);
90	      if (!first?.value) return;
91	
92	      // Strip GS1 FNC1 (ASCII 29 / "GS") so the printable payload is usable.
93	      // For SSCC labels the raw value contains FNC1 between AIs.
94	      const raw = first.value;
95	      const printable = raw.replace(/\x1d/g, '|');
96	      console.log('[scan]', first.type, JSON.stringify(printable));
97	
98	      lockedRef.current = true;
99	      haptic.success();
100	      flash();
101	      findProduct(raw)
102	        .then((existing) => {
103	          if (existing) navigation.replace('Receiving', { barcode: raw });
104	          else navigation.replace('RegisterProduct', { barcode: raw });
105	        })
106	        .catch(() => navigation.replace('RegisterProduct', { barcode: raw }));
107	    },
108	    [navigation, flash]
109	  );
110	
111	  const codeScanner = useCodeScanner({
112	    codeTypes: [...CODE_TYPES],
113	    onCodeScanned,
114	  });
115	
116	  const lineY = linePos.interpolate({ inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: [0, 220] });
117	
118	  if (!hasPermission) {
119	    return (
120	      <SafeAreaView style={styles.permission}>
121	        <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: spacing.md }}>
122	          Camera access needed
123	        </Text>
124	        <Text
125	          variant="bodyLarge"
126	          color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
127	          style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginBottom: spacing.xl }}
128	        >
129	          We use the camera only to read barcodes — no photos are taken.
130	        </Text>
131	        <Button label="Allow camera" onPress={requestPermission} size="lg" fullWidth />
132	        <Button
133	          label="Not now"
134	          variant="text"
135	          onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}
136	          style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}
137	        />
138	      </SafeAreaView>
139	    );
140	  }
141	
142	  if (!device) {
143	    return (
144	      <SafeAreaView style={styles.permission}>
145	        <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
146	          No camera available
147	        </Text>
148	        <Button
149	          label="Back"
150	          variant="text"
151	          onPress={() => navigation.goBack()}
152	          style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}
153	        />
154	      </SafeAreaView>
155	    );
156	  }
157	
158	  return (
159	    <View style={styles.root}>
160	      <Camera
161	        style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
162	        device={device}
163	        isActive
164	        torch={torch ? 'on' : 'off'}
165	        codeScanner={codeScanner}
166	      />
167	
168	      <View style={styles.maskTop} />
169	      <View style={styles.maskRow}>
170	        <View style={styles.maskSide} />
171	        <View style={styles.window}>
172	          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTL]} />
173	          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerTR]} />
174	          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBL]} />
175	          <View style={[styles.corner, styles.cornerBR]} />
176	          <Animated.View style={[styles.scanLine, { transform: [{ translateY: lineY }] }]} />
177	        </View>
178	        <View style={styles.maskSide} />
179	      </View>
180	      <View style={styles.maskBottom} />
181	
182	      <Animated.View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.flash, { opacity: flashOpacity }]} />
183	
184	      <SafeAreaView edges={['top']} style={styles.topBar}>
185	        <View style={styles.topBarRow}>
186	          <IconButton icon="✕" onPress={() => navigation.goBack()} tone="inverse" />
187	          <Text variant="titleMedium" color="#fff">
188	            Scan barcode
189	          </Text>
190	          <IconButton
191	            icon={torch ? '◉' : '○'}
192	            onPress={() => setTorch((t) => !t)}
193	            tone="inverse"
194	          />
195	        </View>
196	      </SafeAreaView>
197	
198	      <SafeAreaView edges={['bottom']} style={styles.hint}>
199	        <Text
200	          variant="bodyLarge"
201	          color="#fff"
202	          style={{ textAlign: 'center', paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl }}
203	        >
204	          Hold steady. Align the barcode inside the frame.
205	        </Text>
206	      </SafeAreaView>
207	    </View>
208	  );
209	}
210	
211	const WINDOW = 260;
212	
213	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
214	  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#000' },
215	  permission: {
216	    flex: 1,
217	    backgroundColor: palette.background,
218	    padding: spacing.xl,
219	    justifyContent: 'center',
220	  },
221	  topBar: { position: 'absolute', top: 0, left: 0, right: 0 },
222	  topBarRow: {
223	    flexDirection: 'row',
224	    alignItems: 'center',
225	    justifyContent: 'space-between',
226	    paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,
227	    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,
228	  },
229	  maskTop: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)' },
230	  maskBottom: { flex: 1.4, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)' },
231	  maskRow: { flexDirection: 'row', height: WINDOW },
232	  maskSide: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)' },
233	  window: { width: WINDOW, height: WINDOW, overflow: 'hidden' },
234	  corner: { position: 'absolute', width: 28, height: 28, borderColor: '#fff' },
235	  cornerTL: { top: 0, left: 0, borderTopWidth: 3, borderLeftWidth: 3, borderTopLeftRadius: 12 },
236	  cornerTR: { top: 0, right: 0, borderTopWidth: 3, borderRightWidth: 3, borderTopRightRadius: 12 },
237	  cornerBL: { bottom: 0, left: 0, borderBottomWidth: 3, borderLeftWidth: 3, borderBottomLeftRadius: 12 },
238	  cornerBR: { bottom: 0, right: 0, borderBottomWidth: 3, borderRightWidth: 3, borderBottomRightRadius: 12 },
239	  scanLine: {
240	    position: 'absolute',
241	    left: 8,
242	    right: 8,
243	    height: 2,
244	    backgroundColor: palette.primaryContainer,
245	    shadowColor: palette.primaryContainer,
246	    shadowOpacity: 1,
247	    shadowRadius: 8,
248	    ...(Platform.OS === 'android' ? { elevation: 4 } : null),
249	  },
250	  flash: { ...StyleSheet.absoluteFillObject, backgroundColor: '#fff' },
251	  hint: { position: 'absolute', bottom: 0, left: 0, right: 0, paddingBottom: spacing.lg },
252	});
253

5	import {
6	  AppBar,
7	  Button,
8	  Card,

11	  spacing,
12	  StatusPill,

14	} from '../design';

16	import { enqueue } from '../db/outbox';
17	import { findProduct, Product } from '../db/products';
18	import { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';
19	import { flushOnce } from '../sync/syncService';
20	import { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';
21	
22	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Receiving'>;
23	
24	export function ReceivingScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
25	  const { barcode } = route.params;
26	  const [product, setProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);
27	  const [item, setItem] = useState<OrderItem | null>(null);
28	  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);
29	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
30	
31	  useEffect(() => {
32	    (async () => {
33	      setProduct(await findProduct(barcode));
34	      setItem(await findOpenItemByBarcode(barcode));
35	    })();
36	  }, [barcode]);
37	
38	  const bump = (n: number) => {
39	    haptic.tap();
40	    setQty((q) => Math.max(1, q + n));
41	  };
42	  const setExact = (n: number) => {
43	    haptic.tap();
44	    setQty(n);
45	  };
46	
47	  const confirm = async () => {
48	    setSaving(true);
49	    try {
50	      const id = `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`;
51	      await enqueue('receipt', {
52	        id,
53	        order_id: item?.order_id ?? null,
54	        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,
55	        barcode,
56	        product_name: product?.name ?? 'Unknown',
57	        qty,
58	        scanned_at: Date.now(),
59	      });
60	      if (item) await addReceivedQty(item.id, qty);
61	      flushOnce().catch(() => {});
62	      haptic.success();
63	      navigation.replace('Scanner');
64	    } finally {
65	      setSaving(false);
66	    }
67	  };
68	
69	  const expected = item?.expected_qty ?? null;
70	  const alreadyIn = item?.received_qty ?? 0;
71	  const projected = alreadyIn + qty;
72	  const mismatch = expected !== null && projected > expected;
73	
74	  return (
75	    <View style={styles.safe}>
76	      <AppBar
77	        title="Receive item"
78	        onBack={() => navigation.popToTop()}
79	        trailing={
80	          expected !== null ? (
81	            <StatusPill
82	              label={`${projected} / ${expected}`}
83	              tone={mismatch ? 'danger' : projected === expected ? 'success' : 'neutral'}
84	            />
85	          ) : null
86	        }
87	      />
88	
89	      <View style={styles.content}>
90	        <Card tone="elevated" padding="xl">
91	          <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
92	            PRODUCT
93	          </Text>
94	          <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
95	            {product?.name ?? 'Unknown product'}
96	          </Text>
97	          <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
98	            {barcode}
99	          </Text>
100	          {expected !== null ? (
101	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
102	              Expected {expected} · {alreadyIn} already received
103	            </Text>
104	          ) : (
105	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
106	              No matching order — capture stands alone.
107	            </Text>
108	          )}
109	        </Card>
110	
111	        <View style={styles.qtySection}>
112	          <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
113	            QUANTITY RECEIVED
114	          </Text>
115	          <View style={styles.stepperRow}>
116	            <Stepper icon="−" onPress={() => bump(-1)} />
117	            <Text variant="displayLarge" style={styles.qtyNum}>
118	              {qty}
119	            </Text>
120	            <Stepper icon="+" onPress={() => bump(1)} />
121	          </View>
122	          <View style={styles.quickRow}>
123	            {[5, 10, 20, 50].map((n) => (
124	              <QuickChip key={n} value={n} active={qty === n} onPress={() => setExact(n)} />
125	            ))}
126	          </View>
127	          {mismatch ? (
128	            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginTop: spacing.md }}>
129	              <StatusPill
130	                label={`Over by ${projected - expected!}`}
131	                tone="danger"
132	                leadingIcon="⚠"
133	              />
134	            </View>
135	          ) : null}
136	        </View>
137	      </View>
138	
139	      <View style={styles.footer}>
140	        <Button
141	          label="Confirm received"
142	          onPress={confirm}
143	          loading={saving}
144	          size="lg"
145	          fullWidth
146	          leadingIcon={
147	            <Text variant="titleLarge" color={palette.onPrimary}>
148	              ✓
149	            </Text>
150	          }
151	        />
152	        <Button
153	          label="Cancel"
154	          variant="text"
155	          onPress={() => navigation.popToTop()}
156	          style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}
157	        />
158	      </View>
159	    </View>
160	  );
161	}
162	
163	function Stepper({ icon, onPress }: { icon: string; onPress: () => void }) {
164	  return (
165	    <Pressable
166	      onPress={onPress}
167	      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true, radius: 44 }}
168	      style={styles.stepper}
169	    >

171	        {icon}
172	      </Text>

191	        {value}
192	      </Text>
193	    </Pressable>
194	  );
195	}
196

1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
3	import { FlatList, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
4	import { AppBar, Card, palette, spacing, StatusPill, Text } from '../design';

8	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Orders'>;
9	type Enriched = Order & { items: OrderItem[] };

11	export function OrdersScreen({ navigation }: Props) {

13	
14	  const load = useCallback(async () => {

18	    );

22	    load();
23	  }, [load]);
24	
25	  return (
26	    <View style={styles.safe}>

28	      <FlatList

30	        keyExtractor={(o) => o.id}
31	        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
32	        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />}
33	        renderItem={({ item }) => {
34	          const done = item.items.every((i) => i.received_qty >= i.expected_qty) && item.items.length > 0;
35	          return (
36	            <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
37	              <View style={styles.head}>

39	                  <Text variant="titleLarge">{item.vendor ?? 'Order'}</Text>

42	                      Due {new Date(item.expected_at).toLocaleDateString()}
43	                    </Text>
44	                  ) : null}
45	                </View>
46	                <StatusPill
47	                  label={done ? 'Complete' : `${item.items.length} item${item.items.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`}
48	                  tone={done ? 'success' : 'neutral'}
49	                />
50	              </View>
51	
52	              <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />
53	              {item.items.map((i, idx) => {
54	                const itemDone = i.received_qty >= i.expected_qty;

58	                    style={[
59	                      styles.itemRow,

63	                    <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
64	                      <Text variant="bodyLarge" color={itemDone ? palette.onSurfaceVariant : palette.onSurface}>

68	                    <Text

73	                    </Text>

75	                );
76	              })}
77	            </Card>
78	          );
79	        }}

83	              No deliveries scheduled

85	            <Text
86	              variant="bodyLarge"
87	              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
88	              style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.sm }}
89	            >
90	              Orders appear here once the office processes a bill.
91	            </Text>
92	          </View>
93	        }
94	      />
95	    </View>
96	  );
97	}

1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import React, { useState } from 'react';
3	import { KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
4	import {
5	  AppBar,
6	  Button,
7	  Chip,
8	  palette,
9	  spacing,
10	  Text,
11	  TextField,
12	} from '../design';
13	import { enqueue } from '../db/outbox';
14	import { upsertProduct } from '../db/products';
15	import { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';
16	import { flushOnce } from '../sync/syncService';
17	import { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';
18	
19	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'RegisterProduct'>;
20	
21	const CATEGORIES = ['Grocery', 'Cleaning', 'Office', 'Cafeteria', 'Other'];
22	const UNITS = ['Piece', 'Kg', 'Litre', 'Pack', 'Box'];
23	
24	export function RegisterProductScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
25	  const { barcode } = route.params;
26	  const [name, setName] = useState('');
27	  const [category, setCategory] = useState('Grocery');
28	  const [unit, setUnit] = useState('Piece');
29	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
30	  const canSave = name.trim().length > 0 && !saving;
31	
32	  const onSave = async () => {
33	    if (!canSave) {
34	      haptic.warn();
35	      return;
36	    }
37	    setSaving(true);
38	    try {
39	      await upsertProduct({ barcode, name: name.trim(), category, unit });
40	      await enqueue('product_registration', { barcode, name: name.trim(), category, unit });
41	      flushOnce().catch(() => {});
42	      haptic.success();
43	      navigation.replace('Receiving', { barcode });
44	    } finally {
45	      setSaving(false);
46	    }
47	  };
48	
49	  return (
50	    <View style={styles.safe}>
51	      <AppBar title="New product" subtitle={barcode} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
52	      <KeyboardAvoidingView
53	        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
54	        style={{ flex: 1 }}
55	      >
56	        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scroll} keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled">
57	          <Text variant="headlineMedium">What is this?</Text>
58	          <Text variant="bodyLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
59	            Tell us once. We'll remember it next time you scan it.
60	          </Text>
61	
62	          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl }}>
63	            <TextField
64	              label="Product name"
65	              value={name}
66	              onChangeText={setName}
67	              placeholder="e.g. Tata Salt 1kg"
68	              autoFocus
69	              returnKeyType="done"
70	            />
71	          </View>
72	
73	          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl }}>
74	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
75	              CATEGORY
76	            </Text>
77	            <View style={styles.chipRow}>
78	              {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (
79	                <Chip key={c} label={c} selected={c === category} onPress={() => setCategory(c)} />
80	              ))}
81	            </View>
82	          </View>
83	
84	          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl }}>
85	            <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>
86	              UNIT
87	            </Text>
88	            <View style={styles.chipRow}>
89	              {UNITS.map((u) => (
90	                <Chip key={u} label={u} selected={u === unit} onPress={() => setUnit(u)} />
91	              ))}
92	            </View>
93	          </View>
94	        </ScrollView>
95	
96	        <View style={styles.footer}>
97	          <Button
98	            label="Save & continue"
99	            onPress={onSave}
100	            disabled={!canSave}
101	            loading={saving}
102	            size="lg"
103	            fullWidth
104	          />
105	          <Button
106	            label="Cancel"
107	            variant="text"
108	            onPress={() => navigation.popToTop()}
109	            style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}
110	          />
111	        </View>
112	      </KeyboardAvoidingView>
113	    </View>
114	  );
115	}
116

1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
3	import { FlatList, RefreshControl, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
4	import { AppBar, Button, Card, palette, spacing, StatusPill, Text } from '../design';
5	import { getDb } from '../db/database';
6	import { OutboxRow } from '../db/outbox';
7	import { RootStackParamList } from '../navigation/types';
8	import { flushOnce } from '../sync/syncService';
9	
10	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Outbox'>;
11	
12	const KIND_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
13	  receipt: 'Receipt',
14	  product_registration: 'Product mapping',
15	};
16	
17	const TONE: Record<OutboxRow['status'], 'success' | 'warn' | 'danger' | 'neutral'> = {
18	  sent: 'success',
19	  sending: 'warn',
20	  failed: 'danger',
21	  queued: 'neutral',
22	};
23	
24	export function OutboxScreen({ navigation }: Props) {
25	  const [rows, setRows] = useState<OutboxRow[]>([]);
26	  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
27	
28	  const load = useCallback(async () => {
29	    const db = await getDb();
30	    setRows(
31	      await db.getAllAsync<OutboxRow>(
32	        'SELECT * FROM outbox ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 200'
33	      )
34	    );
35	  }, []);
36	
37	  useEffect(() => {
38	    load();
39	  }, [load]);
40	
41	  const sync = async () => {
42	    setBusy(true);
43	    try {
44	      await flushOnce();
45	      await load();
46	    } finally {
47	      setBusy(false);
48	    }
49	  };
50	
51	  return (
52	    <View style={styles.safe}>
53	      <AppBar title="Sync queue" onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />
54	      <FlatList
55	        data={rows}
56	        keyExtractor={(r) => r.id}
57	        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
58	        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={busy} onRefresh={sync} tintColor={palette.primary} />}
59	        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.sm }} />}
60	        renderItem={({ item }) => (
61	          <Card tone="filled" padding="lg">
62	            <View style={styles.rowHead}>
63	              <Text variant="titleMedium">{KIND_LABEL[item.kind] ?? item.kind}</Text>
64	              <StatusPill label={item.status} tone={TONE[item.status]} />
65	            </View>
66	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
67	              {new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleString()} · {item.attempts} attempt
68	              {item.attempts === 1 ? '' : 's'}
69	            </Text>
70	            {item.last_error ? (
71	              <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.error} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
72	                {item.last_error}
73	              </Text>
74	            ) : null}
75	          </Card>
76	        )}
77	        ListEmptyComponent={
78	          <View style={styles.empty}>
79	            <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
80	              Nothing in the queue
81	            </Text>
82	            <Text
83	              variant="bodyLarge"
84	              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}
85	              style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.sm }}
86	            >
87	              Everything you've scanned has been sent.
88	            </Text>
89	          </View>
90	        }
91	      />
92	      <View style={styles.footer}>
93	        <Button label="Sync now" onPress={sync} loading={busy} size="lg" fullWidth />
94	      </View>
95	    </View>
96	  );
97	}

1	import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
2	import { StatusPill } from '../design';
3	import { onPendingChange } from '../sync/syncService';
4	
5	export function QueueBadge() {
6	  const [count, setCount] = useState(0);
7	  useEffect(() => onPendingChange(setCount), []);
8	  if (count === 0) return <StatusPill label="All synced" tone="success" leadingIcon="✓" />;
9	  return <StatusPill label={`${count} waiting to send`} tone="warn" leadingIcon="↻" />;
10	}
11

  "content": "import { palette as steadyPurplePalette } from '../design/tokens';\nimport type { Theme, ThemeName } from './types';\n\n// The default brand theme. Mirrors the values exported from `design/tokens.ts`\n// so any not-yet-migrated component that imports `palette` directly still\n// resolves to the same colors as `useTheme()` consumers.\nconst steadyPurple: Theme = {\n  name: 'steadyPurple',\n  label: 'Steady',\n  palette: steadyPurplePalette,\n};\n\n// Placeholder for the design's \"Bold\" variant — chunky/expressive variant\n// from the design bundle. The shape matches Steady; values are tuned for\n// later (see design/tokens.jsx in the handoff bundle). Kept here so the\n// ThemeProvider machinery has a real second theme to switch to once the\n// remaining components fully consume `useTheme()`.\nconst bold: Theme = {\n  name: 'bold',\n  label: 'Bold',\n  palette: {\n    ...steadyPurplePalette,\n    background: '#FBF8F1',\n    surface: '#FFFFFF',\n    surfaceContainerLow: '#FDFAF2',\n    surfaceContainer: '#F1EDE2',\n    onSurfaceVariant: '#5A4F33',\n    outlineVariant: '#15110A',\n  },\n};\n\nexport const THEMES: Record<ThemeName, Theme> = {\n  steadyPurple,\n  bold,\n};\n\nexport const DEFAULT_THEME: ThemeName = 'steadyPurple';\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/theme/ThemeProvider.tsx",
  "content": "import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';\nimport React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport { DEFAULT_THEME, THEMES } from './themes';\nimport type { Theme, ThemeName } from './types';\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'app.theme';\n\ntype Ctx = {\n  theme: Theme;\n  themeName: ThemeName;\n  setTheme: (name: ThemeName) => void;\n};\n\nconst ThemeContext = createContext<Ctx | null>(null);\n\nexport function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  const [themeName, setThemeName] = useState<ThemeName>(DEFAULT_THEME);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY).then((v) => {\n      if (v && v in THEMES) setThemeName(v as ThemeName);\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const setTheme = useCallback((name: ThemeName) => {\n    setThemeName(name);\n    AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, name).catch(() => {});\n  }, []);\n\n  const value = useMemo<Ctx>(\n    () => ({ theme: THEMES[themeName], themeName, setTheme }),\n    [themeName, setTheme]\n  );\n\n  return <ThemeContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</ThemeContext.Provider>;\n}\n\nexport function useTheme(): Theme {\n  const ctx = useContext(ThemeContext);\n  if (!ctx) {\n    // Fallback to default so non-wrapped code paths (e.g. tests) don't crash.\n    return THEMES[DEFAULT_THEME];\n  }\n  return ctx.theme;\n}\n\nexport function useThemeControls() {\n  const ctx = useContext(ThemeContext);\n  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useThemeControls must be inside ThemeProvider');\n  return ctx;\n}\n"
}

stockSub:        ['Check shelf counts', 'शेल्फ़ गिनती'],\n  issue:           ['Issue',              'देना'],\n  issueSub:        ['Record what was used','खर्च दर्ज करें'],\n  alerts: {
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/i18n/I18nProvider.tsx",
  "content": "import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';\nimport React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport { Lang, StringKey, translate } from './strings';\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'app.lang';\nconst DEFAULT_LANG: Lang = 'en';\n\ntype Ctx = {\n  lang: Lang;\n  setLang: (l: Lang) => void;\n  /** Translate a string key. Unknown keys are returned verbatim. */\n  t: (key: StringKey) => string;\n  /** True when the user has selected icons-only mode. */\n  iconsOnly: boolean;\n};\n\nconst I18nContext = createContext<Ctx | null>(null);\n\nexport function I18nProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<Lang>(DEFAULT_LANG);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY).then((v) => {\n      if (v === 'en' || v === 'hi' || v === 'icons') setLangState(v);\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const setLang = useCallback((l: Lang) => {\n    setLangState(l);\n    AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, l).catch(() => {});\n  }, []);\n\n  const value = useMemo<Ctx>(\n    () => ({\n      lang,\n      setLang,\n      t: (key) => translate(key, lang),\n      iconsOnly: lang === 'icons',\n    }),\n    [lang, setLang]\n  );\n\n  return <I18nContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</I18nContext.Provider>;\n}\n\nexport function useI18n(): Ctx {\n  const ctx = useContext(I18nContext);\n  if (!ctx) {\n    // Safe fallback when called outside the provider (e.g. tests).\n    return {\n      lang: DEFAULT_LANG,\n      setLang: () => {},\n      t: (key) => translate(key, DEFAULT_LANG),\n      iconsOnly: false,\n    };\n  }\n  return ctx;\n}\n\n/** Convenience hook: returns just the `t` function. */\nexport function useT() {\n  return useI18n().t;\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/rbac/permissions.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * RBAC stub. Phase 1: there is no auth yet, so `useCan()` returns true for\n * everything. The shape is real, though — modules can already declare the\n * permissions they require, and screens can call `useCan()` to gate UI.\n *\n * When auth lands, replace the implementation with a real check against the\n * authenticated user's role/permission set. The contract here doesn't change.\n */\n\nimport { useCallback } from 'react';\n\n// Permissions are namespaced strings: `<module>.<verb>` or `<module>.<resource>.<verb>`.\n// Keep them short; resolve them against a real policy engine later.\nexport type Permission = string;\n\nexport type Role = 'guard' | 'supervisor' | 'manager' | 'admin';\n\nexport type Principal = {\n  userId: string;\n  roles: Role[];\n  permissions: Permission[];\n};\n\n// Placeholder principal. Replace with the real signed-in user once auth lands.\nexport const ANONYMOUS_PRINCIPAL: Principal = {\n  userId: 'anon',\n  roles: ['guard'],\n  permissions: ['*'],\n};\n\nexport function can(principal: Principal, permission: Permission): boolean {\n  if (principal.permissions.includes('*')) return true;\n  return principal.permissions.includes(permission);\n}\n\n/**\n * Hook form for use in components. Returns a stable checker so callers can\n * do `const can = useCan(); can('inventory.receive')`.\n */\nexport function useCan() {\n  return useCallback((permission: Permission) => can(ANONYMOUS_PRINCIPAL, permission), []);\n}\n\nexport function useHasPermission(permission: Permission): boolean {\n  return useCan()(permission);\n}\n"
}
```

{

  "content": "import type { StringKey } from '../i18n';\nimport type { Permission } from '../rbac';\n\n/**\n * A Module is a self-contained feature area (inventory, attendance,\n * maintenance, …). Each module declares its home-screen tile, the route it\n * lands on, the navigator screens it owns, and the permissions a user must\n * hold to see and enter it.\n *\n * Modules live under `src/modules/<id>/` with a single `index.ts` exporting\n * the Module object. Add a new module by importing it into the registry.\n */\nexport type ModuleId = 'inventory' | 'attendance' | 'dailyTasks' | 'maintenance';\n\nexport type ModuleScreen = {\n  /** Route name as it appears in the navigator. */\n  name: string;\n  /** React component for the screen. Typed loose to avoid leaking\n   * RootStackParamList details into the registry. */\n  component: React.ComponentType<any>;\n  /** Override default screen options for this route. */\n  options?: Record<string, unknown>;\n};\n\nexport type Module = {\n  id: ModuleId;\n  /** i18n key for the module's display name. */\n  titleKey: StringKey;\n  /** i18n key for the home-tile subtitle. */\n  subtitleKey?: StringKey;\n  /** Short emoji/glyph used by the launcher tile until proper icons land. */\n  glyph: string;\n  /** Route the home tile navigates to. */\n  entryRoute: string;\n  /** Permissions a user must hold to see this module on the home screen. */\n  requiredPermissions: Permission[];\n  /** Screens this module contributes to the root navigator. */\n  screens: ModuleScreen[];\n};\n"
}

  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/navigation/RootNavigator.tsx",
  "content": "import { DefaultTheme, NavigationContainer } from '@react-navigation/native';\nimport { createNativeStackNavigator } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport React from 'react';\nimport { getAllScreens } from '../modules';\nimport { useTheme } from '../theme';\n\nconst Stack = createNativeStackNavigator();\n\nexport function RootNavigator() {\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n\n  const navTheme = {\n    ...DefaultTheme,\n    colors: {\n      ...DefaultTheme.colors,\n      primary: palette.primary,\n      background: palette.background,\n      card: palette.surface,\n      text: palette.onSurface,\n      border: palette.outlineVariant,\n      notification: palette.error,\n    },\n  };\n\n  const screens = getAllScreens();\n\n  return (\n    <NavigationContainer theme={navTheme}>\n      <Stack.Navigator\n        screenOptions={{\n          headerShown: false,\n          contentStyle: { backgroundColor: palette.background },\n          animation: 'slide_from_right',\n        }}\n      >\n        {screens.map((s) => (\n          <Stack.Screen\n            key={s.name}\n            name={s.name}\n            component={s.component}\n            options={s.options as any}\n          />\n        ))}\n      </Stack.Navigator>\n    </NavigationContainer>\n  );\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';\nimport React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { GestureHandlerRootView } from 'react-native-gesture-handler';\nimport { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';\nimport { Text } from './src/design';\nimport { getDb } from './src/db/database';\nimport { I18nProvider } from './src/i18n';\nimport { RootNavigator } from './src/navigation/RootNavigator';\nimport { startSync, stopSync } from './src/sync/syncService';\nimport { ThemeProvider, useTheme } from './src/theme';\n\nexport default function App() {\n  return (\n    <ThemeProvider>\n      <I18nProvider>\n        <AppShell />\n      </I18nProvider>\n    </ThemeProvider>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction AppShell() {\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    (async () => {\n      try {\n        await getDb();\n        startSync();\n        setReady(true);\n      } catch (e) {\n        setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));\n      }\n    })();\n    return () => stopSync();\n  }, []);\n\n  if (error) {\n    return (\n      <View style={[styles.center, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>\n        <Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" color={palette.error}>\n          Startup failed\n        </Text>\n        <Text\n          variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n          color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n          style={{ marginTop: 8, textAlign: 'center' }}\n        >\n          {error}\n        </Text>\n      </View>\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (!ready) {\n    return (\n      <View style={[styles.center, { backgroundColor: palette.background }]}>\n        <ActivityIndicator color={palette.primary} size=\"large\" />\n      </View>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <GestureHandlerRootView style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n      <SafeAreaProvider>\n        <StatusBar style=\"dark\" />\n        <RootNavigator />\n      </SafeAreaProvider>\n    </GestureHandlerRootView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  center: {\n    flex: 1,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n    padding: 24,\n  },\n});\n"
}

> AGENT

Foundation landed and `tsc --noEmit` is green. Summary of what's in:

**New foundation**
- `src/theme/` — `ThemeProvider` + `useTheme()` + two themes (`steadyPurple` is default and uses `#92288E` as primary; `bold` placeholder matches the design's second variant). Choice persists in AsyncStorage.
- `src/i18n/` — `I18nProvider` + `useT()` + the EN/HI string table lifted from the design bundle. Lang persists; `iconsOnly` flag for icons-only mode.
- `src/rbac/` — `Permission`/`Principal` types, `useCan()` hook returning a `(perm) => boolean` checker. Stub principal currently grants `*`.
- `src/modules/` — `Module` type + registry pattern. Adding a new module = one file + one line in `registry.ts`.

**Migration**
- Existing screens moved via `git mv` into `src/modules/inventory/screens/` (history preserved).
- `QueueBadge` moved to `src/modules/inventory/components/`.
- Old `src/screens/` and `src/components/` directories removed.
- `inventoryModule` declares its tile metadata, entry route, permissions (`inventory.view`), and contributes its screens to the root navigator.
- `RootNavigator` now mounts screens from `getAllScreens()` — adding a module auto-mounts its screens.
- `App.tsx` wraps the tree in `ThemeProvider` + `I18nProvider`.

**Visible changes**
- Brand primary swapped from green `#0B6E3E` to `#92288E` across the palette (containers/secondary/background recoloured for tonal consistency). All other UI behaviour and layout is unchanged. The two `Button` pressed-state colors that were hardcoded for the old green are now hardcoded for the new purple.

**Deferred to follow-up**
- Migrating each design component from the static `palette` import to `useTheme()` — works as-is because the static `palette` and the default theme's `palette` are the same object.
- Wiring `useT()` into existing screens (English copy unchanged for now).
- New screens (Stock, Issue, Alerts) + receiving-flow extensions (mismatch, proof photo, summary).

Ready to move on to step 3 (new screens + receiving extensions) when you are.

5	export async function getDb(): Promise<SQLite.SQLiteDatabase> {
6	  if (dbInstance) return dbInstance;
7	  const db = await SQLite.openDatabaseAsync('fieldapp.db');
8	  await db.execAsync(`
9	    PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;
10	    PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;
11	
12	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS products (
13	      barcode TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
14	      name TEXT NOT NULL,
15	      category TEXT,
16	      unit TEXT,
17	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
18	    );
19	
20	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS orders (
21	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
22	      vendor TEXT,
23	      expected_at INTEGER,
24	      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',
25	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
26	    );
27	
28	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS order_items (
29	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
30	      order_id TEXT NOT NULL,
31	      barcode TEXT,
32	      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
33	      expected_qty REAL NOT NULL,
34	      received_qty REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
35	      FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
36	    );
37	
38	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS receipts (
39	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
40	      order_id TEXT,
41	      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,
42	      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,
43	      qty REAL NOT NULL,
44	      scanned_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
45	      note TEXT
46	    );
47	
48	    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS outbox (
49	      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
50	      kind TEXT NOT NULL,
51	      payload TEXT NOT NULL,
52	      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued',
53	      attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
54	      last_error TEXT,
55	      created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,
56	      updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL
57	    );
58	
59	    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_outbox_status ON outbox(status);
60	    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_order_items_order ON order_items(order_id);
61	  `);
62	  dbInstance = db;
63	  return db;
64	}
65	
66	export function now(): number {
67	  return Date.now();
68	}

1	import { getDb, now } from './database';

3	export type Product = {
4	  barcode: string;
5	  name: string;
6	  category: string | null;
7	  unit: string | null;
8	  updated_at: number;
9	};
10	
11	export async function findProduct(barcode: string): Promise<Product | null> {
12	  const db = await getDb();
13	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<Product>(
14	    'SELECT * FROM products WHERE barcode = ?',
15	    [barcode]
16	  );
17	  return row ?? null;
18	}
19	
20	export async function upsertProduct(p: Omit<Product, 'updated_at'>): Promise<Product> {
21	  const db = await getDb();
22	  const ts = now();
23	  await db.runAsync(
24	    `INSERT INTO products (barcode, name, category, unit, updated_at)
25	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
26	     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET
27	       name=excluded.name, category=excluded.category, unit=excluded.unit, updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,
28	    [p.barcode, p.name, p.category, p.unit, ts]
29	  );
30	  return { ...p, updated_at: ts };
31	}
32	
33	export async function listProducts(limit = 200): Promise<Product[]> {
34	  const db = await getDb();
35	  return db.getAllAsync<Product>(
36	    'SELECT * FROM products ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT ?',
37	    [limit]
38	  );
39	}

1	import { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';
2	import { getDb, now } from './database';
3	
4	export type OutboxKind = 'receipt' | 'product_registration';
5	
6	export type OutboxRow = {
7	  id: string;
8	  kind: OutboxKind;
9	  payload: string;
10	  status: 'queued' | 'sending' | 'sent' | 'failed';
11	  attempts: number;
12	  last_error: string | null;
13	  created_at: number;
14	  updated_at: number;
15	};
16	
17	export async function enqueue(kind: OutboxKind, payload: object): Promise<string> {
18	  const db = await getDb();
19	  const id = `out_${nanoid(12)}`;
20	  const ts = now();
21	  await db.runAsync(
22	    `INSERT INTO outbox (id, kind, payload, status, attempts, created_at, updated_at)
23	     VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'queued', 0, ?, ?)`,
24	    [id, kind, JSON.stringify(payload), ts, ts]
25	  );
26	  return id;
27	}
28	
29	export async function pendingCount(): Promise<number> {
30	  const db = await getDb();
31	  const row = await db.getFirstAsync<{ c: number }>(
32	    `SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM outbox WHERE status IN ('queued', 'failed')`
33	  );
34	  return row?.c ?? 0;
35	}
36	
37	export async function nextBatch(limit: number): Promise<OutboxRow[]> {
38	  const db = await getDb();
39	  return db.getAllAsync<OutboxRow>(
40	    `SELECT * FROM outbox WHERE status IN ('queued', 'failed')
41	     ORDER BY created_at ASC LIMIT ?`,
42	    [limit]
43	  );
44	}
45	
46	export async function markSending(id: string): Promise<void> {
47	  const db = await getDb();
48	  await db.runAsync(
49	    `UPDATE outbox SET status = 'sending', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,
50	    [now(), id]
51	  );
52	}
53	
54	export async function markSent(id: string): Promise<void> {
55	  const db = await getDb();
56	  await db.runAsync(
57	    `UPDATE outbox SET status = 'sent', updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,
58	    [now(), id]
59	  );
60	}
61	
62	export async function markFailed(id: string, err: string): Promise<void> {
63	  const db = await getDb();
64	  await db.runAsync(
65	    `UPDATE outbox SET status = 'failed', attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`,
66	    [err.slice(0, 500), now(), id]
67	  );
68	}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/db/stock.ts",
  "content": "import { getDb, now } from './database';\n\nexport type StockStatus = 'ok' | 'low' | 'out';\n\nexport type StockRow = {\n  barcode: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string | null;\n  unit: string | null;\n  on_hand: number;\n  threshold: number;\n  updated_at: number;\n};\n\nexport function statusFor(row: Pick<StockRow, 'on_hand' | 'threshold'>): StockStatus {\n  if (row.on_hand <= 0) return 'out';\n  if (row.on_hand <= row.threshold) return 'low';\n  return 'ok';\n}\n\nexport async function listStock(): Promise<StockRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  return db.getAllAsync<StockRow>('SELECT * FROM stock_levels ORDER BY name COLLATE NOCASE ASC');\n}\n\nexport async function listLowOrOut(): Promise<StockRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  return db.getAllAsync<StockRow>(\n    `SELECT * FROM stock_levels WHERE on_hand <= threshold ORDER BY on_hand ASC, name COLLATE NOCASE ASC`\n  );\n}\n\nexport async function findStock(barcode: string): Promise<StockRow | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  return (\n    (await db.getFirstAsync<StockRow>('SELECT * FROM stock_levels WHERE barcode = ?', [barcode])) ??\n    null\n  );\n}\n\nexport async function upsertStock(row: {\n  barcode: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string | null;\n  unit: string | null;\n  on_hand?: number;\n  threshold?: number;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.runAsync(\n    `INSERT INTO stock_levels (barcode, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold, updated_at)\n     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n     ON CONFLICT(barcode) DO UPDATE SET\n       name=excluded.name, category=excluded.category, unit=excluded.unit, updated_at=excluded.updated_at`,\n    [\n      row.barcode,\n      row.name,\n      row.category,\n      row.unit,\n      row.on_hand ?? 0,\n      row.threshold ?? 0,\n      now(),\n    ]\n  );\n}\n\nexport async function adjustOnHand(barcode: string, delta: number): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.runAsync(\n    `UPDATE stock_levels SET on_hand = MAX(0, on_hand + ?), updated_at = ? WHERE barcode = ?`,\n    [delta, now(), barcode]\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Seed demo stock once so Stock/Alerts/Issue screens show something on a\n * fresh install. Idempotent: skipped if any stock row already exists.\n */\nexport async function seedDemoStockIfEmpty(): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const existing = await db.getFirstAsync<{ c: number }>(\n    'SELECT COUNT(*) as c FROM stock_levels'\n  );\n  if ((existing?.c ?? 0) > 0) return;\n  const ts = now();\n  const seed: Array<[string, string, string, string, number, number]> = [\n    ['8901030875021', 'A4 Printer Paper',     'Stationery', 'Pack',  12, 6],\n    ['8901001234567', 'Blue Ballpoint Pens',  'Stationery', 'Box',    3, 5],\n    ['8902080000034', 'Floor Cleaner 1L',     'Cleaning',   'Litre',  0, 4],\n    ['8901491100012', 'Toilet Paper Rolls',   'Cleaning',   'Pack',   8, 8],\n    ['8901058000023', 'Tea Bags (100ct)',     'Pantry',     'Box',    5, 4],\n    ['8901491307041', 'Coffee Powder 200g',   'Pantry',     'Piece',  1, 3],\n    ['8901030698778', 'HDMI Cable 1.8m',      'IT supplies','Piece',  2, 2],\n    ['8901138514525', 'USB-C Charger 20W',    'IT supplies','Piece',  6, 3],\n  ];\n  for (const [barcode, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold] of seed) {\n    await db.runAsync(\n      `INSERT INTO stock_levels (barcode, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold, updated_at)\n       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,\n      [barcode, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold, ts]\n    );\n  }\n}\n"
}
```

  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/screens/StockScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport { FlatList, StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from 'react-native';\nimport {\n  AppBar,\n  Card,\n  Chip,\n  palette,\n  radius,\n  spacing,\n  StatusPill,\n  Text,\n} from '../../../design';\nimport { listStock, statusFor, StockRow, StockStatus } from '../../../db/stock';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Stock'>;\n\nconst CATEGORIES = ['All', 'Stationery', 'Cleaning', 'Pantry', 'IT supplies'] as const;\n\nconst STATUS_TONE: Record<StockStatus, 'success' | 'warn' | 'danger'> = {\n  ok: 'success',\n  low: 'warn',\n  out: 'danger',\n};\n\nconst STATUS_LABEL: Record<StockStatus, string> = {\n  ok: 'In stock',\n  low: 'Low',\n  out: 'Out',\n};\n\nexport function StockScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const [rows, setRows] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');\n  const [category, setCategory] = useState<(typeof CATEGORIES)[number]>('All');\n\n  const load = useCallback(async () => {\n    setRows(await listStock());\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load();\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', load);\n    return unsub;\n  }, [navigation, load]);\n\n  const filtered = useMemo(() => {\n    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();\n    return rows.filter((r) => {\n      if (category !== 'All' && r.category !== category) return false;\n      if (!q) return true;\n      return r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.barcode.includes(q);\n    });\n  }, [rows, query, category]);\n\n  return (\n    <View style={styles.safe}>\n      <AppBar title={t('stock')} subtitle={t('stockSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n\n      <View style={styles.search}>\n        <TextInput\n          value={query}\n          onChangeText={setQuery}\n          placeholder={t('search')}\n          placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n          style={styles.searchInput}\n        />\n      </View>\n\n      <View style={styles.chips}>\n        {CATEGORIES.map((c) => (\n          <Chip key={c} label={c} selected={c === category} onPress={() => setCategory(c)} />\n        ))}\n      </View>\n\n      <FlatList\n        data={filtered}\n        keyExtractor={(r) => r.barcode}\n        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}\n        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.sm }} />}\n        renderItem={({ item }) => {\n          const status = statusFor(item);\n          return (\n            <Card tone=\"filled\" padding=\"lg\">\n              <View style={styles.row}>\n                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n                  <Text variant=\"titleMedium\">{item.name}</Text>\n                  <Text\n                    variant=\"bodyMedium\"\n                    color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n                    style={{ marginTop: 2 }}\n                  >\n                    {item.category ?? '—'} · {item.on_hand} {item.unit ?? ''} {t('onHand')}\n                  </Text>\n                </View>\n                <StatusPill label={STATUS_LABEL[status]} tone={STATUS_TONE[status]} />\n              </View>\n            </Card>\n          );\n        }}\n        ListEmptyComponent={\n          <View style={styles.empty}>\n            <Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>\n              Nothing matches\n            </Text>\n            <Text\n              variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n              style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.sm }}\n            >\n              Try a different category or clear the search.\n            </Text>\n          </View>\n        }\n      />\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  search: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.sm },\n  searchInput: {\n    backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    borderWidth: 1.5,\n    borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,\n    minHeight: 52,\n    color: palette.onSurface,\n    fontSize: 17,\n  },\n  chips: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    flexWrap: 'wrap',\n    gap: spacing.sm,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl,\n    paddingTop: spacing.md,\n    paddingBottom: spacing.sm,\n  },\n  list: { padding: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.md, paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },\n  row: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.md },\n  empty: { padding: spacing.xxl },\n});\n"
}
