{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/screens/IssueScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';\nimport React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport {\n  FlatList,\n  KeyboardAvoidingView,\n  Platform,\n  Pressable,\n  StyleSheet,\n  TextInput,\n  View,\n} from 'react-native';\nimport {\n  AppBar,\n  Button,\n  Card,\n  Chip,\n  palette,\n  radius,\n  spacing,\n  Text,\n  TextField,\n} from '../../../design';\nimport { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';\nimport { adjustOnHand, findStock, listStock, StockRow } from '../../../db/stock';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';\nimport { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';\nimport { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Issue'>;\n\nconst REASONS = ['Office use', 'Pantry', 'Cleaning', 'Maintenance', 'Other'];\n\nexport function IssueScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const initialBarcode = route.params?.barcode;\n  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<StockRow | null>(null);\n  const [all, setAll] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');\n  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);\n  const [reason, setReason] = useState(REASONS[0]);\n  const [who, setWho] = useState('');\n  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    listStock().then(setAll);\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (initialBarcode) {\n      findStock(initialBarcode).then((r) => r && setSelected(r));\n    }\n  }, [initialBarcode]);\n\n  const results = useMemo(() => {\n    if (selected) return [];\n    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();\n    if (!q) return all.slice(0, 25);\n    return all\n      .filter((r) => r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || r.barcode.includes(q))\n      .slice(0, 25);\n  }, [all, query, selected]);\n\n  const bump = useCallback((d: number) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    setQty((v) => Math.max(1, v + d));\n  }, []);\n\n  const submit = async () => {\n    if (!selected) return;\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await enqueue('issue', {\n        id: `iss_${nanoid(12)}`,\n        barcode: selected.barcode,\n        product_name: selected.name,\n        qty,\n        reason,\n        taken_by: who.trim() || null,\n        issued_at: Date.now(),\n      });\n      await adjustOnHand(selected.barcode, -qty);\n      flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.goBack();\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  if (!selected) {\n    return (\n      <View style={styles.safe}>\n        <AppBar title={t('issue')} subtitle={t('issueSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n        <View style={styles.search}>\n          <TextInput\n            value={query}\n            onChangeText={setQuery}\n            placeholder={t('search')}\n            placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n            style={styles.searchInput}\n            autoFocus\n          />\n        </View>\n        <FlatList\n          data={results}\n          keyExtractor={(r) => r.barcode}\n          contentContainerStyle={styles.list}\n          ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.sm }} />}\n          renderItem={({ item }) => (\n            <Card tone=\"filled\" padding=\"lg\" onPress={() => setSelected(item)}>\n              <Text variant=\"titleMedium\">{item.name}</Text>\n              <Text\n                variant=\"bodyMedium\"\n                color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n                style={{ marginTop: 2 }}\n              >\n                {item.category ?? '—'} · {item.on_hand} {item.unit ?? ''} {t('onHand')}\n              </Text>\n            </Card>\n          )}\n          ListEmptyComponent={\n            <View style={styles.empty}>\n              <Text variant=\"bodyLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>\n                No items match.\n              </Text>\n            </View>\n          }\n        />\n      </View>\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <View style={styles.safe}>\n      <AppBar\n        title={t('issue')}\n        subtitle={selected.name}\n        onBack={() => setSelected(null)}\n      />\n      <KeyboardAvoidingView\n        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}\n        style={{ flex: 1 }}\n      >\n        <View style={styles.body}>\n          <Card tone=\"elevated\" padding=\"xl\">\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              PRODUCT\n            </Text>\n            <Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n              {selected.name}\n            </Text>\n            <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n              {selected.on_hand} {selected.unit ?? ''} {t('onHand')}\n            </Text>\n          </Card>\n\n          <View style={styles.qtySection}>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>\n              {t('howMany').toUpperCase()}\n            </Text>\n            <View style={styles.stepperRow}>\n              <Stepper icon=\"−\" onPress={() => bump(-1)} />\n              <Text variant=\"displayLarge\" style={styles.qtyNum}>{qty}</Text>\n              <Stepper icon=\"+\" onPress={() => bump(1)} />\n            </View>\n          </View>\n\n          <View>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.sm }}>\n              {t('reason').toUpperCase()}\n            </Text>\n            <View style={styles.chipRow}>\n              {REASONS.map((r) => (\n                <Chip key={r} label={r} selected={r === reason} onPress={() => setReason(r)} />\n              ))}\n            </View>\n          </View>\n\n          <TextField\n            label={t('whoTook')}\n            value={who}\n            onChangeText={setWho}\n            placeholder=\"Name (optional)\"\n            returnKeyType=\"done\"\n          />\n        </View>\n\n        <View style={styles.footer}>\n          <Button\n            label={t('confirm')}\n            onPress={submit}\n            loading={saving}\n            size=\"lg\"\n            fullWidth\n            leadingIcon={\n              <Text variant=\"titleLarge\" color={palette.onPrimary}>✓</Text>\n            }\n          />\n        </View>\n      </KeyboardAvoidingView>\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nfunction Stepper({ icon, onPress }: { icon: string; onPress: () => void }) {\n  return (\n    <Pressable\n      onPress={onPress}\n      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true, radius: 44 }}\n      style={styles.stepper}\n    >\n      <Text variant=\"displayMedium\" color={palette.onPrimary}>{icon}</Text>\n    </Pressable>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  search: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.sm },\n  searchInput: {\n    backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    borderWidth: 1.5,\n    borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,\n    minHeight: 52,\n    color: palette.onSurface,\n    fontSize: 17,\n  },\n  list: { padding: spacing.xl, paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },\n  body: { flex: 1, padding: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.xl },\n  qtySection: { gap: spacing.lg },\n  stepperRow: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n    gap: spacing.xl,\n  },\n  stepper: {\n    width: 88,\n    height: 88,\n    borderRadius: 44,\n    backgroundColor: palette.primary,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n    overflow: 'hidden',\n  },\n  qtyNum: { minWidth: 120, textAlign: 'center' },\n  chipRow: { flexDirection: 'row', flexWrap: 'wrap', gap: spacing.sm },\n  empty: { padding: spacing.xxl },\n  footer: {\n    padding: spacing.xl,\n    backgroundColor: palette.surface,\n    borderTopWidth: 1,\n    borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n  },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/screens/AlertsScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';\nimport React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { FlatList, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport {\n  AppBar,\n  Button,\n  Card,\n  palette,\n  spacing,\n  StatusPill,\n  Text,\n} from '../../../design';\nimport { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';\nimport { listLowOrOut, statusFor, StockRow } from '../../../db/stock';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';\nimport { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';\nimport { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Alerts'>;\n\nexport function AlertsScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const [rows, setRows] = useState<StockRow[]>([]);\n  const [requested, setRequested] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());\n\n  const load = useCallback(async () => {\n    setRows(await listLowOrOut());\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load();\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', load);\n    return unsub;\n  }, [navigation, load]);\n\n  const reorder = async (row: StockRow) => {\n    haptic.tap();\n    await enqueue('reorder_request', {\n      id: `ror_${nanoid(12)}`,\n      barcode: row.barcode,\n      product_name: row.name,\n      on_hand: row.on_hand,\n      threshold: row.threshold,\n      requested_at: Date.now(),\n    });\n    setRequested((prev) => new Set(prev).add(row.barcode));\n    flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n    haptic.success();\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <View style={styles.safe}>\n      <AppBar title={t('alerts')} subtitle={t('alertsSub')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n      <FlatList\n        data={rows}\n        keyExtractor={(r) => r.barcode}\n        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}\n        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />}\n        renderItem={({ item }) => {\n          const status = statusFor(item);\n          const tone = status === 'out' ? 'danger' : 'warn';\n          const label = status === 'out' ? t('outStock') : t('lowStock');\n          const done = requested.has(item.barcode);\n          return (\n            <Card tone=\"filled\" padding=\"lg\">\n              <View style={styles.head}>\n                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n                  <Text variant=\"titleMedium\">{item.name}</Text>\n                  <Text\n                    variant=\"bodyMedium\"\n                    color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n                    style={{ marginTop: 2 }}\n                  >\n                    {item.on_hand} {item.unit ?? ''} {t('onHand')} · threshold {item.threshold}\n                  </Text>\n                </View>\n                <StatusPill label={label} tone={tone} />\n              </View>\n              <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />\n              {done ? (\n                <StatusPill label={t('reorderSent')} tone=\"success\" leadingIcon=\"✓\" />\n              ) : (\n                <Button\n                  label={t('requestReorder')}\n                  variant=\"tonal\"\n                  size=\"md\"\n                  onPress={() => reorder(item)}\n                />\n              )}\n            </Card>\n          );\n        }}\n        ListEmptyComponent={\n          <View style={styles.empty}>\n            <Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>\n              All stocked up\n            </Text>\n            <Text\n              variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n              style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.sm }}\n            >\n              Nothing is below its threshold right now.\n            </Text>\n          </View>\n        }\n      />\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  list: { padding: spacing.xl, paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },\n  head: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: spacing.md },\n  empty: { padding: spacing.xxl },\n});\n"
}

NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'DeliverySummary'>;\n\nexport function DeliverySummaryScreen({ navigation, route }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { productName, qty, expected, flagged } = route.params;\n  const matched = expected !== null && qty === expected;\n\n  return (\n    <View style={styles.safe}>\n      <AppBar title={t('deliveryDone')} />\n      <View style={styles.body}>\n        <View style={styles.hero}>\n          <View style={styles.tick}>\n            <Text variant=\"displayMedium\" color={palette.onPrimary}>✓</Text>\n          </View>\n          <Text variant=\"headlineMedium\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.lg, textAlign: 'center' }}>\n            {t('deliveryDone')}\n          </Text>\n          <Text\n            variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n            color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n            style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm, textAlign: 'center' }}\n          >\n            {t('willSyncLater')}\n          </Text>\n        </View>\n\n        <Card tone=\"elevated\" padding=\"xl\">\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n            PRODUCT\n          </Text>\n          <Text variant=\"titleLarge\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n            {productName}\n          </Text>\n          <View style={styles.rowBetween}>\n            <Text variant=\"bodyLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              {t('scanned')}\n            </Text>\n            <Text variant=\"titleMedium\">{qty}</Text>\n          </View>\n          {expected !== null && (\n            <View style={styles.rowBetween}>\n              <Text variant=\"bodyLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n                {t('expected')}\n              </Text>\n              <Text variant=\"titleMedium\">{expected}</Text>\n            </View>\n          )}\n          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>\n            {flagged ? (\n              <StatusPill label={t('mismatch')} tone=\"danger\" leadingIcon=\"⚠\" />\n            ) : matched ? (\n              <StatusPill label=\"Counts match\" tone=\"success\" leadingIcon=\"✓\" />\n            ) : (\n              <StatusPill label=\"Standalone receipt\" tone=\"neutral\" />\n            )}\n          </View>\n        </Card>\n      </View>\n\n      <View style={styles.footer}>\n        <Button\n          label={t('startScanning')}\n          variant=\"tonal\"\n          size=\"lg\"\n          fullWidth\n          onPress={() => navigation.replace('Scanner')}\n        />\n        <Button\n          label={t('backHome')}\n          variant=\"text\"\n          onPress={() => navigation.popToTop()}\n          style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}\n        />\n      </View>\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  body: { flex: 1, padding: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.xl },\n  hero: { alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: spacing.lg },\n  tick: {\n    width: 96,\n    height: 96,\n    borderRadius: 48,\n    backgroundColor: palette.primary,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n  },\n  rowBetween: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    justifyContent: 'space-between',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    marginTop: spacing.md,\n  },\n  footer: {\n    padding: spacing.xl,\n    backgroundColor: palette.surface,\n    borderTopWidth: 1,\n    borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n  },\n});\n"
}

'Unknown',\n      qty,\n      expected: item?.expected_qty ?? null,\n      flagged,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const confirm = async () => {\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await persist(false);\n      haptic.success();\n      goToSummary(false);\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const flagAndContinue = async () => {\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await persist(true);\n      haptic.warn();\n      goToSummary(true);\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };"

1	import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';
2	import { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';
3	import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
4	import { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';
5	import {
6	  AppBar,
7	  Button,
8	  Card,
9	  palette,
10	  radius,
11	  spacing,
12	  StatusPill,
13	  Text,
14	} from '../../../design';
15	import { addReceivedQty, findOpenItemByBarcode, OrderItem } from '../../../db/orders';
16	import { enqueue } from '../../../db/outbox';
17	import { findProduct, Product } from '../../../db/products';
18	import { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';
19	import { flushOnce } from '../../../sync/syncService';
20	import { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';
21	
22	type Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Receiving'>;
23	
24	export function ReceivingScreen({ route, navigation }: Props) {
25	  const { barcode } = route.params;
26	  const [product, setProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);
27	  const [item, setItem] = useState<OrderItem | null>(null);
28	  const [qty, setQty] = useState(1);
29	  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
30	
31	  useEffect(() => {
32	    (async () => {
33	      setProduct(await findProduct(barcode));
34	      setItem(await findOpenItemByBarcode(barcode));
35	    })();
36	  }, [barcode]);
37	
38	  const bump = (n: number) => {
39	    haptic.tap();
40	    setQty((q) => Math.max(1, q + n));
41	  };
42	  const setExact = (n: number) => {
43	    haptic.tap();
44	    setQty(n);
45	  };
46	
47	  const confirm = async () => {
48	    setSaving(true);
49	    try {
50	      const id = `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`;
51	      await enqueue('receipt', {
52	        id,
53	        order_id: item?.order_id ?? null,
54	        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,
55	        barcode,
56	        product_name: product?.name ?? 'Unknown',
57	        qty,
58	        scanned_at: Date.now(),
59	      });
60	      if (item) await addReceivedQty(item.id, qty);
61	      flushOnce().catch(() => {});
62	      haptic.success();
63	      navigation.replace('Scanner');
64	    } finally {
65	      setSaving(false);
66	    }
67	  };
68	
69	  const expected = item?.expected_qty ?? null;
70	  const alreadyIn = item?.received_qty ?? 0;
71	  const projected = alreadyIn + qty;
72	  const mismatch = expected !== null && projected > expected;
73	
74	  return (
75	    <View style={styles.safe}>
76	      <AppBar
77	        title="Receive item"
78	        onBack={() => navigation.popToTop()}
79	        trailing={
80	          expected !== null ? (
81	            <StatusPill
82	              label={`${projected} / ${expected}`}
83	              tone={mismatch ? 'danger' : projected === expected ? 'success' : 'neutral'}
84	            />
85	          ) : null
86	        }
87	      />
88	
89	      <View style={styles.content}>
90	        <Card tone="elevated" padding="xl">
91	          <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>
92	            PRODUCT
93	          </Text>
94	          <Text variant="headlineSmall" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
95	            {product?.name ?? 'Unknown product'}
96	          </Text>
97	          <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>
98	            {barcode}
99	          </Text>
100	          {expected !== null ? (
101	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
102	              Expected {expected} · {alreadyIn} already received
103	            </Text>
104	          ) : (
105	            <Text variant="bodyMedium" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>
106	              No matching order — capture stands alone.
107	            </Text>
108	          )}
109	        </Card>
110	
111	        <View style={styles.qtySection}>
112	          <Text variant="labelLarge" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>
113	            QUANTITY RECEIVED
114	          </Text>
115	          <View style={styles.stepperRow}>
116	            <Stepper icon="−" onPress={() => bump(-1)} />
117	            <Text variant="displayLarge" style={styles.qtyNum}>
118	              {qty}
119	            </Text>
120	            <Stepper icon="+" onPress={() => bump(1)} />
121	          </View>
122	          <View style={styles.quickRow}>
123	            {[5, 10, 20, 50].map((n) => (
124	              <QuickChip key={n} value={n} active={qty === n} onPress={() => setExact(n)} />
125	            ))}
126	          </View>
127	          {mismatch ? (
128	            <View style={{ alignItems: 'center', marginTop: spacing.md }}>
129	              <StatusPill
130	                label={`Over by ${projected - expected!}`}
131	                tone="danger"
132	                leadingIcon="⚠"
133	              />
134	            </View>
135	          ) : null}
136	        </View>
137	      </View>
138	
139	      <View style={styles.footer}>
140	        <Button
141	          label="Confirm received"
142	          onPress={confirm}
143	          loading={saving}
144	          size="lg"
145	          fullWidth
146	          leadingIcon={
147	            <Text variant="titleLarge" color={palette.onPrimary}>
148	              ✓
149	            </Text>
150	          }
151	        />
152	        <Button
153	          label="Cancel"
154	          variant="text"
155	          onPress={() => navigation.popToTop()}
156	          style={{ marginTop: spacing.sm }}
157	        />
158	      </View>
159	    </View>
160	  );
161	}
162	
163	function Stepper({ icon, onPress }: { icon: string; onPress: () => void }) {
164	  return (
165	    <Pressable
166	      onPress={onPress}
167	      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true, radius: 44 }}
168	      style={styles.stepper}
169	    >
170	      <Text variant="displayMedium" color={palette.onPrimary}>
171	        {icon}
172	      </Text>
173	    </Pressable>
174	  );
175	}
176	
177	function QuickChip({ value, active, onPress }: { value: number; active: boolean; onPress: () => void }) {
178	  return (
179	    <Pressable
180	      onPress={onPress}
181	      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant }}
182	      style={[
183	        styles.quickChip,
184	        {
185	          backgroundColor: active ? palette.secondaryContainer : palette.surfaceContainerLow,
186	          borderColor: active ? palette.secondaryContainer : palette.outlineVariant,
187	        },
188	      ]}
189	    >
190	      <Text variant="titleMedium" color={active ? palette.onSecondaryContainer : palette.onSurface}>
191	        {value}
192	      </Text>
193	    </Pressable>
194	  );
195	}
196	
197	const styles = StyleSheet.create({
198	  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },
199	  content: { flex: 1, padding: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.xl },
200	  qtySection: { gap: spacing.lg, alignItems: 'stretch' },
201	  stepperRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', gap: spacing.xl },
202	  stepper: {
203	    width: 88,
204	    height: 88,
205	    borderRadius: 44,
206	    backgroundColor: palette.primary,
207	    alignItems: 'center',
208	    justifyContent: 'center',
209	    overflow: 'hidden',
210	  },
211	  qtyNum: { minWidth: 120, textAlign: 'center' },
212	  quickRow: { flexDirection: 'row', gap: spacing.sm, justifyContent: 'center' },
213	  quickChip: {
214	    minWidth: 64,
215	    paddingVertical: spacing.sm,
216	    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,
217	    borderRadius: radius.pill,
218	    borderWidth: 1,
219	    alignItems: 'center',
220	    overflow: 'hidden',
221	  },
222	  footer: {
223	    padding: spacing.xl,
224	    backgroundColor: palette.surface,
225	    borderTopWidth: 1,
226	    borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant,
227	  },
228	});
229

'Unknown',\n        qty,\n        scanned_at: Date.now(),\n      });\n      if (item) await addReceivedQty(item.id, qty);\n      flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n      haptic.success();\n      navigation.replace('Scanner');\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };",
  "new_string": "  const persist = async (flagged: boolean) => {\n    const id = `rcp_${nanoid(12)}`;\n    await enqueue('receipt', {\n      id,\n      order_id: item?.order_id ?? null,\n      order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,\n      barcode,\n      product_name: product?.name ?? 'Unknown',\n      qty,\n      flagged,\n      scanned_at: Date.now(),\n    });\n    if (flagged) {\n      await enqueue('mismatch_flag', {\n        id: `flg_${nanoid(12)}`,\n        receipt_id: id,\n        order_item_id: item?.id ?? null,\n        barcode,\n        expected: item?.expected_qty ?? null,\n        received_total: (item?.received_qty ?? 0) + qty,\n        flagged_at: Date.now(),\n      });\n    }\n    if (item) await addReceivedQty(item.id, qty);\n\n    // Roll the received qty into on-hand stock. Create the row if this\n    // product hasn't been tracked before so it shows up on Stock/Alerts.\n    const existing = await findStock(barcode);\n    if (existing) {\n      await adjustOnHand(barcode, qty);\n    } else {\n      await upsertStock({\n        barcode,\n        name: product?.name ?? 'Unknown',\n        category: product?.category ?? null,\n        unit: product?.unit ?? null,\n        on_hand: qty,\n        threshold: 0,\n      });\n    }\n\n    flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n  };\n\n  const goToSummary = (flagged: boolean) => {\n    navigation.replace('DeliverySummary', {\n      productName: product?.name ?? 'Unknown',\n      qty,\n      expected: item?.expected_qty ?? null,\n      flagged,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const confirm = async () => {\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await persist(false);\n      haptic.success();\n      goToSummary(false);\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const flagAndContinue = async () => {\n    setSaving(true);\n    try {\n      await persist(true);\n      haptic.warn();\n      goToSummary(true);\n    } finally {\n      setSaving(false);\n    }\n  };"
}

  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';\nimport { SectionList, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { AppBar, Card, palette, spacing, StatusPill, Text } from '../../../design';\nimport { getOrderItems, listOpenOrders, Order, OrderItem } from '../../../db/orders';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Orders'>;\ntype Enriched = Order & { items: OrderItem[] };\ntype Bucket = 'arrived' | 'awaited' | 'done';\n\nfunction bucketOf(o: Enriched): Bucket {\n  if (o.items.length === 0) return 'awaited';\n  const allDone = o.items.every((i) => i.received_qty >= i.expected_qty);\n  if (allDone) return 'done';\n  const anyReceived = o.items.some((i) => i.received_qty > 0);\n  return anyReceived ? 'arrived' : 'awaited';\n}\n\nconst SECTION_ORDER: Bucket[] = ['arrived', 'awaited', 'done'];\n\nexport function OrdersScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Enriched[]>([]);\n\n  const load = useCallback(async () => {\n    const list = await listOpenOrders();\n    setOrders(\n      await Promise.all(list.map(async (o) => ({ ...o, items: await getOrderItems(o.id) })))\n    );\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load();\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', load);\n    return unsub;\n  }, [navigation, load]);\n\n  const sections = useMemo(() => {\n    const groups: Record<Bucket, Enriched[]> = { arrived: [], awaited: [], done: [] };\n    for (const o of orders) groups[bucketOf(o)].push(o);\n    const titleFor: Record<Bucket, string> = {\n      arrived: 'Arrived',\n      awaited: t('awaited'),\n      done: t('doneToday'),\n    };\n    return SECTION_ORDER.filter((b) => groups[b].length > 0).map((b) => ({\n      title: titleFor[b],\n      bucket: b,\n      data: groups[b],\n    }));\n  }, [orders, t]);\n\n  return (\n    <View style={styles.safe}>\n      <AppBar title={t('receiving')} subtitle={t('expectedToday')} onBack={() => navigation.goBack()} />\n      <SectionList\n        sections={sections}\n        keyExtractor={(o) => o.id}\n        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}\n        stickySectionHeadersEnabled={false}\n        renderSectionHeader={({ section }) => (\n          <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              {section.title.toUpperCase()} · {section.data.length}\n            </Text>\n          </View>\n        )}\n        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />}\n        SectionSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: spacing.lg }} />}\n        renderItem={({ item, section }) => {\n          const bucket = (section as unknown as { bucket: Bucket }).bucket;\n          return (\n            <Card tone=\"filled\" padding=\"lg\">\n              <View style={styles.head}>\n                <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n                  <Text variant=\"titleLarge\">{item.vendor ?? 'Order'}</Text>\n                  {item.expected_at ? (\n                    <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n                      Due {new Date(item.expected_at).toLocaleDateString()}\n                    </Text>\n                  ) : null}\n                </View>\n                <StatusPill\n                  label={\n                    bucket === 'done'\n                      ? t('done')\n                      : `${item.items.length} ${t('items')}`\n                  }\n                  tone={bucket === 'done' ? 'success' : bucket === 'arrived' ? 'warn' : 'neutral'}\n                />\n              </View>\n\n              <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />\n              {item.items.map((i, idx) => {\n                const itemDone = i.received_qty >= i.expected_qty;\n                return (\n                  <View\n                    key={i.id}\n                    style={[\n                      styles.itemRow,\n                      idx < item.items.length - 1 && styles.itemRowDivider,\n                    ]}\n                  >\n                    <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>\n                      <Text variant=\"bodyLarge\" color={itemDone ? palette.onSurfaceVariant : palette.onSurface}>\n                        {i.product_name}\n                      </Text>\n                    </View>\n                    <Text\n                      variant=\"titleMedium\"\n                      color={itemDone ? palette.primary : palette.onSurface}\n                    >\n                      {i.received_qty} / {i.expected_qty}\n                    </Text>\n                  </View>\n                );\n              })}\n            </Card>\n          );\n        }}\n        ListEmptyComponent={\n          <View style={styles.empty}>\n            <Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>\n              No deliveries scheduled\n            </Text>\n            <Text\n              variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n              style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.sm }}\n            >\n              Orders appear here once the office processes a bill.\n            </Text>\n          </View>\n        }\n      />\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  list: { padding: spacing.xl, paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },\n  sectionHeader: { paddingBottom: spacing.sm },\n  head: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'flex-start', gap: spacing.md },\n  itemRow: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', paddingVertical: spacing.sm, gap: spacing.md },\n  itemRowDivider: { borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: palette.outlineVariant },\n  empty: { padding: spacing.xxl },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/modules/inventory/screens/HomeScreen.tsx",
  "content": "import { NativeStackScreenProps } from '@react-navigation/native-stack';\nimport React, { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';\nimport { Button, Card, palette, spacing, Text } from '../../../design';\nimport { listOpenOrders } from '../../../db/orders';\nimport { listProducts } from '../../../db/products';\nimport { listLowOrOut } from '../../../db/stock';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { RootStackParamList } from '../../../navigation/types';\nimport { QueueBadge } from '../components/QueueBadge';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Home'>;\n\ntype Stats = {\n  orders: number;\n  products: number;\n  alerts: number;\n};\n\nexport function HomeScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats>({ orders: 0, products: 0, alerts: 0 });\n\n  const load = useCallback(async () => {\n    const [p, o, a] = await Promise.all([listProducts(1000), listOpenOrders(), listLowOrOut()]);\n    setStats({ products: p.length, orders: o.length, alerts: a.length });\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load();\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', load);\n    return unsub;\n  }, [navigation, load]);\n\n  return (\n    <SafeAreaView edges={['top']} style={styles.safe}>\n      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scroll}>\n        <View style={styles.header}>\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n            {t('guard').toUpperCase()}\n          </Text>\n          <Text variant=\"displayMedium\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n            {t('appName')}\n          </Text>\n          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>\n            <QueueBadge />\n          </View>\n        </View>\n\n        <View style={styles.primary}>\n          <Button\n            label={t('startScanning')}\n            leadingIcon={<Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" color={palette.onPrimary}>⌖</Text>}\n            size=\"xl\"\n            fullWidth\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Scanner')}\n          />\n          <Text\n            variant=\"bodyMedium\"\n            color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n            style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.md }}\n          >\n            {t('scanItem')}.\n          </Text>\n        </View>\n\n        <View style={styles.tilesRow}>\n          <Tile\n            label={t('receiving')}\n            value={stats.orders}\n            sub={t('expectedToday')}\n            glyph=\"🚚\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Orders')}\n          />\n          <Tile\n            label={t('stock')}\n            value={stats.products}\n            sub={t('stockSub')}\n            glyph=\"📦\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Stock')}\n          />\n        </View>\n\n        <View style={styles.tilesRow}>\n          <Tile\n            label={t('issue')}\n            sub={t('issueSub')}\n            glyph=\"✋\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Issue')}\n          />\n          <Tile\n            label={t('alerts')}\n            value={stats.alerts || undefined}\n            sub={t('alertsSub')}\n            glyph=\"🔔\"\n            tone={stats.alerts > 0 ? 'warn' : 'neutral'}\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Alerts')}\n          />\n        </View>\n\n        <View style={styles.tilesRow}>\n          <Tile\n            label=\"Sync queue\"\n            sub=\"Review pending uploads\"\n            glyph=\"↻\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Outbox')}\n          />\n          <View style={{ flex: 1 }} />\n        </View>\n      </ScrollView>\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\ntype TileProps = {\n  label: string;\n  value?: number;\n  sub: string;\n  glyph: string;\n  tone?: 'neutral' | 'warn';\n  onPress: () => void;\n};\n\nfunction Tile({ label, value, sub, glyph, tone = 'neutral', onPress }: TileProps) {\n  return (\n    <Card tone=\"filled\" onPress={onPress} style={styles.tile}>\n      <View style={styles.tileHeader}>\n        <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n          {label.toUpperCase()}\n        </Text>\n        <Text variant=\"titleLarge\">{glyph}</Text>\n      </View>\n      {value !== undefined ? (\n        <Text\n          variant=\"displayMedium\"\n          color={tone === 'warn' ? palette.error : palette.onSurface}\n          style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}\n        >\n          {value}\n        </Text>\n      ) : (\n        <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />\n      )}\n      <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n        {sub}\n      </Text>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  scroll: { paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },\n  header: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.lg, paddingBottom: spacing.xl },\n  primary: {\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl,\n    paddingTop: spacing.md,\n    paddingBottom: spacing.xxl,\n  },\n  tilesRow: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    gap: spacing.md,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl,\n    marginBottom: spacing.md,\n  },\n  tile: { flex: 1 },\n  tileHeader: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' },\n});\n"
}

from '../../../navigation/types';\nimport { QueueBadge } from '../components/QueueBadge';\n\ntype Props = NativeStackScreenProps<RootStackParamList, 'Home'>;\n\ntype Stats = {\n  orders: number;\n  products: number;\n  alerts: number;\n};\n\nexport function HomeScreen({ navigation }: Props) {\n  const t = useT();\n  const [stats, setStats] = useState<Stats>({ orders: 0, products: 0, alerts: 0 });\n\n  const load = useCallback(async () => {\n    const [p, o, a] = await Promise.all([listProducts(1000), listOpenOrders(), listLowOrOut()]);\n    setStats({ products: p.length, orders: o.length, alerts: a.length });\n  }, []);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    load();\n    const unsub = navigation.addListener('focus', load);\n    return unsub;\n  }, [navigation, load]);\n\n  return (\n    <SafeAreaView edges={['top']} style={styles.safe}>\n      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scroll}>\n        <View style={styles.header}>\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n            {t('guard').toUpperCase()}\n          </Text>\n          <Text variant=\"displayMedium\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n            {t('appName')}\n          </Text>\n          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}>\n            <QueueBadge />\n          </View>\n        </View>\n\n        <View style={styles.primary}>\n          <Button\n            label={t('startScanning')}\n            leadingIcon={<Text variant=\"headlineSmall\" color={palette.onPrimary}>⌖</Text>}\n            size=\"xl\"\n            fullWidth\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Scanner')}\n          />\n          <Text\n            variant=\"bodyMedium\"\n            color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n            style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: spacing.md }}\n          >\n            {t('scanItem')}.\n          </Text>\n        </View>\n\n        <View style={styles.tilesRow}>\n          <Tile\n            label={t('receiving')}\n            value={stats.orders}\n            sub={t('expectedToday')}\n            glyph=\"🚚\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Orders')}\n          />\n          <Tile\n            label={t('stock')}\n            value={stats.products}\n            sub={t('stockSub')}\n            glyph=\"📦\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Stock')}\n          />\n        </View>\n\n        <View style={styles.tilesRow}>\n          <Tile\n            label={t('issue')}\n            sub={t('issueSub')}\n            glyph=\"✋\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Issue')}\n          />\n          <Tile\n            label={t('alerts')}\n            value={stats.alerts || undefined}\n            sub={t('alertsSub')}\n            glyph=\"🔔\"\n            tone={stats.alerts > 0 ? 'warn' : 'neutral'}\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Alerts')}\n          />\n        </View>\n\n        <View style={styles.tilesRow}>\n          <Tile\n            label=\"Sync queue\"\n            sub=\"Review pending uploads\"\n            glyph=\"↻\"\n            onPress={() => navigation.navigate('Outbox')}\n          />\n          <View style={{ flex: 1 }} />\n        </View>\n      </ScrollView>\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\ntype TileProps = {\n  label: string;\n  value?: number;\n  sub: string;\n  glyph: string;\n  tone?: 'neutral' | 'warn';\n  onPress: () => void;\n};\n\nfunction Tile({ label, value, sub, glyph, tone = 'neutral', onPress }: TileProps) {\n  return (\n    <Card tone=\"filled\" onPress={onPress} style={styles.tile}>\n      <View style={styles.tileHeader}>\n        <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n          {label.toUpperCase()}\n        </Text>\n        <Text variant=\"titleLarge\">{glyph}</Text>\n      </View>\n      {value !== undefined ? (\n        <Text\n          variant=\"displayMedium\"\n          color={tone === 'warn' ? palette.error : palette.onSurface}\n          style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}\n        >\n          {value}\n        </Text>\n      ) : (\n        <View style={{ height: spacing.md }} />\n      )}\n      <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n        {sub}\n      </Text>\n    </Card>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  scroll: { paddingBottom: spacing.xxxl },\n  header: { paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl, paddingTop: spacing.lg, paddingBottom: spacing.xl },\n  primary: {\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl,\n    paddingTop: spacing.md,\n    paddingBottom: spacing.xxl,\n  },\n  tilesRow: {\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    gap: spacing.md,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.xl,\n    marginBottom: spacing.md,\n  },\n  tile: { flex: 1 },\n  tileHeader: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' },\n});\n"
}

> AGENT

Step 3 is in and `tsc --noEmit` is green. Here's what shipped:

**New screens**
- `StockScreen` — search bar + category chips (All/Stationery/Cleaning/Pantry/IT) + list of stock rows with in/low/out status pills.
- `IssueScreen` — search-to-pick (or pre-filled by `?barcode`), big +/− stepper, reason chips, optional "who took it" field. Enqueues an `issue` outbox row and decrements `on_hand`.
- `AlertsScreen` — lists everything at/below threshold, inline "Request reorder" button that enqueues a `reorder_request` and flips to a "Request sent" pill.
- `DeliverySummaryScreen` — post-confirm summary card with product, scanned vs. expected, and a Counts-match / Mismatch / Standalone pill. CTAs to keep scanning or go home.

**Receiving flow extensions**
- When the projected qty exceeds `expected_qty`, the primary CTA swaps to a `danger`-styled **Flag & continue** button. Confirms still go through, but additionally enqueue a `mismatch_flag` outbox row and pass `flagged: true` into the summary.
- Both confirm paths roll the received qty into `stock_levels` (`adjustOnHand`, or `upsertStock` for previously-untracked barcodes) so the new Stock/Alerts screens reflect actual receipts.
- Replaces the old `navigate(Scanner)` end-state with the new `DeliverySummary`.

**Orders screen**
- Now a `SectionList` grouped Arrived / Awaited / Done, derived from item-level `received_qty` vs `expected_qty`. Empty buckets are hidden.

**Home**
- New tile launcher: Receiving · Stock · Issue · Alerts · Sync queue. Counts live-load on focus (orders, products mapped, low/out alerts). Alert tile turns red and shows count when alerts > 0. Strings come through `useT()`.

**Data + plumbing**
- New `stock_levels` table created in the same `getDb()` initializer (`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` — safe on existing installs).
- `seedDemoStockIfEmpty()` runs once at boot so the new screens have something to render on a fresh install.
- `OutboxKind` extended: `issue`, `reorder_request`, `mismatch_flag`. `OutboxScreen` labels updated. The sync `api.ts` already dispatches by `kind` strings, so new kinds just need server-side handlers when the backend catches up — no client change required to send them.

**Backend contract additions** (so the server team can mirror):
- `POST /v1/capture/issue` — body: `{id, barcode, product_name, qty, reason, taken_by, issued_at}`
- `POST /v1/capture/reorder_request` — body: `{id, barcode, product_name, on_hand, threshold, requested_at}`
- `POST /v1/capture/mismatch_flag` — body: `{id, receipt_id, order_item_id, barcode, expected, received_total, flagged_at}`

**Deferred (not in this pass)**
- Proof-of-delivery photo capture screen (needs an image-capture flow beyond the existing vision-camera barcode usage).
- Voice-prompt audio (the button hook exists in the design; no audio assets yet).
- Cross-module launcher (only meaningful once a second module — attendance/daily-tasks/maintenance — gets added).
- Wiring `useT()` through the older screens that still use English literals (RegisterProduct, Outbox, Receiving body strings) — purely cosmetic; the i18n hook is ready when you want to switch.

# Appointy Ops Architecture Guide

This document is the canonical architecture and guardrails reference for coding AI agents working in this repository.

## Core Vision

Appointy Ops is the **central intelligence backend** for office operations.

- The long-term value lives in the intelligence layer: ingestion, normalization, routing, extraction, memory, automation, search, and orchestration.
- Messaging apps, dashboards, PWAs, APIs, and external tools are **channels** into or out of that intelligence layer.
- Business capabilities such as visitor management, inventory management, maintenance, kitchen services, and future operational systems should behave like **plugins** attached to the core, so they can evolve independently without destabilizing the backbone.

## Architectural Decisions

### 1. Core stays platform-agnostic

The core should not care whether a message came from WhatsApp, Telegram, a web form, voice input, email, or a future channel.

Core responsibilities include:

- standardized message contracts
- orchestration and routing
- shared registries
- scheduling and notifications
- shared storage abstractions
- shared AI workflows and utilities

In this repo, that mostly maps to:

- `src/mastra/index.ts`
- `src/mastra/core/`
- `src/mastra/workflows/`
- shared utilities in `src/mastra/lib/`

### 2. Channels are adapters, not business systems

Channels are just transport and interface layers.

They should be responsible for:

- receiving platform-specific events
- converting them into standardized internal messages
- sending replies or notifications back out
- handling channel-specific auth, media, formatting, and delivery quirks

They should not become the home for business workflows, domain rules, or office-operations logic.

In this repo, channels live in:

- `src/mastra/channels/telegram/`
- `src/mastra/channels/whatsapp/`

### 3. Operational systems should be plugin-like modules

Visitor management, inventory, maintenance, kitchen services, facility operations, and similar bounded systems should be treated as independent product modules attached to the central intelligence backend.

Each system should own its own:

- business rules
- routes and request handlers
- background jobs
- internal helpers
- persistence logic when it is specific to that system
- external integrations that are specific to that system

The goal is that a system can grow, be refactored, or even be extracted later with minimal impact on the core.

The intended extension boundary in this repo is:

- `src/mastra/plugins/`

### 4. Domains define AI understanding

`domains/` are the AI-routing and structured-extraction definitions used by the intelligence pipeline.

A domain definition should describe:

- what kind of message belongs to that domain
- what structured fields should be extracted
- which agent handles the domain
- how the workflow maps extracted data into downstream systems

In other words:

- **plugins** represent bounded product/back-end systems
- **domains** represent the AI classification and extraction model of the world

A mature operational area may need both a plugin and a domain.

### 5. Build for independent evolution

New operational areas should be added in a way that minimizes changes to shared infrastructure.

Preferred approach:

1. Add or extend a plugin for the bounded system.
2. Add or extend a domain only if the AI pipeline needs to classify or extract that type of work.
3. Touch the core only when the capability is truly shared across multiple systems or channels.

## Boundaries By Folder

### `src/mastra/core`

Put shared primitives and orchestration here:

- message routing
- scheduling
- batch processing
- channel-neutral notifications
- channel-neutral types and contracts
- shared registries

Do not put:

- WhatsApp- or Telegram-specific behavior
- visitor-only, inventory-only, or kitchen-only business rules

### `src/mastra/channels`

Put channel adapters here:
---AGENTS---
# AGENTS.md

You are a TypeScript developer experienced with the Mastra framework. You build AI agents, tools, workflows, and scorers. You follow strict TypeScript practices and always consult up-to-date Mastra documentation before making changes.

## CRITICAL: Load `mastra` skill

**BEFORE doing ANYTHING with Mastra, load the `mastra` skill FIRST.** Never rely on cached knowledge as Mastra's APIs change frequently between versions. Use the skill to read up-to-date documentation from `node_modules`.

## Project Overview

This is a **Mastra** project written in TypeScript. Mastra is a framework for building AI-powered applications and agents with a modern TypeScript stack. The Node.js runtime is `>=22.13.0`.

## Architecture Context

Read `ARCHITECTURE.md` before making structural changes.

Key architectural intent:

- `appointy-ops` is the central intelligence backend
- messaging apps, PWAs, dashboards, and external tools are channels
- operational systems like visitor management, inventory, maintenance, and kitchen services should be developed as plugin-like modules with minimal impact on core
- preserve channel-agnostic and plugin-friendly boundaries whenever possible

## Commands

```bash
npm run dev # Start Mastra Studio at localhost:4111 (long-running, use a separate terminal)
npm run build # Build a production-ready server
```

## Project Structure

| Folder                 | Description                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/mastra`           | Entry point for all Mastra-related code and configuration.                                                                               |
| `src/mastra/agents`    | Define and configure your agents - their behavior, goals, and tools.                                                                     |
| `src/mastra/workflows` | Define multi-step workflows that orchestrate agents and tools together.                                                                  |
| `src/mastra/tools`     | Create reusable tools that your agents can call                                                                                          |
| `src/mastra/mcp`       | (Optional) Implement custom MCP servers to share your tools with external agents                                                         |
| `src/mastra/scorers`   | (Optional) Define scorers for evaluating agent performance over time                                                                     |
| `src/mastra/public`    | (Optional) Contents are copied into the `.build/output` directory during the build process, making them available for serving at runtime |

### Top-level files

Top-level files define how your Mastra project is configured, built, and connected to its environment.

| File                  | Description                                                                                                       |
| --------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `src/mastra/index.ts` | Central entry point where you configure and initialize Mastra.                                                    |
| `.env.example`        | Template for environment variables - copy and rename to `.env` to add your secret [model provider](/models) keys. |
| `package.json`        | Defines project metadata, dependencies, and available npm scripts.                                                |
| `tsconfig.json`       | Configures TypeScript options such as path aliases, compiler settings, and build output.                          |

## Boundaries

### Always do

- Load the `mastra` skill before any Mastra-related work
- Read `ARCHITECTURE.md` before making architecture or module-boundary changes
- Register new agents, tools, workflows, and scorers in `src/mastra/index.ts`
- Use schemas for tool inputs and outputs
- Run `npm run build` to verify changes compile

### Never do

- Never commit `.env` files or secrets
- Never modify `node_modules` or Mastra's database files directly
- Never hardcode API keys (always use environment variables)

## Resources

- [Mastra Documentation](https://mastra.ai/llms.txt)
- [Mastra .well-known skills discovery](https://mastra.ai/.well-known/skills/index.json)

# System Plugins

This folder is the backend extension boundary for product-like systems that live inside `appointy-ops` without becoming part of the core runtime.

## Why this exists

- Keep shared infrastructure in `core/`, `channels/`, and Mastra registration.
- Keep bounded business logic in a plugin-owned surface.
- Make it easy to extract a system later without rewriting the whole app.
- Give future systems like visitor, inventory, or asset operations one obvious home.

## Current pattern

Each plugin gets:

- A registry entry in `src/mastra/plugins/index.ts`
- A plugin definition implementing `OfficeOpsPlugin`
- A single import surface like `src/mastra/plugins/visitor/index.ts`
- Ownership of its own HTTP routes, background jobs, and business helpers

Core code should import plugin functionality from `src/mastra/plugins/<plugin>` instead of reaching into internal module paths.

## Recommended shape for future systems

```text
src/mastra/plugins/
  inventory/
    index.ts
    plugin.ts
    ...
  visitor/
    index.ts
    plugin.ts
    ...
```

If a system also has its own frontend, keep that frontend in a separate client repository:

```text
appointy-visitor-web/
appointy-ops-dashboard/
```

That lets this repository stay focused on backend APIs, orchestration, plugins, channels, and data.
---registry---
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "http";
import type { OfficeOpsPlugin } from "./types";

const plugins = new Map<string, OfficeOpsPlugin>();

export function registerPlugin(plugin: OfficeOpsPlugin): void {
  if (plugins.has(plugin.id)) {
    console.warn(`⚠️ [PluginRegistry] Overwriting existing plugin: ${plugin.id}`);
  }

  plugins.set(plugin.id, plugin);
  console.log(`🧩 [PluginRegistry] Registered plugin: ${plugin.id} (${plugin.name})`);
}

export function getPlugin(id: string): OfficeOpsPlugin | undefined {
  return plugins.get(id);
}

export function getAllPlugins(): OfficeOpsPlugin[] {
  return Array.from(plugins.values());
}

export async function initPlugins(): Promise<void> {
  for (const plugin of plugins.values()) {
    if (plugin.init) {
      await plugin.init();
    }
  }
}

export function startPluginBackgroundJobs(): void {
  for (const plugin of plugins.values()) {
    plugin.startBackgroundJobs?.();
  }
}

export async function handlePluginHttpRequest(
  req: IncomingMessage,
  res: ServerResponse,
): Promise<boolean> {
  for (const plugin of plugins.values()) {
    if (!plugin.handleHttpRequest) continue;

    const handled = await plugin.handleHttpRequest(req, res);
    if (handled) return true;
  }

  return false;
}
---types---
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "http";

export interface OfficeOpsPlugin {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  init?: () => Promise<void>;
  handleHttpRequest?: (
    req: IncomingMessage,
    res: ServerResponse,
  ) => Promise<boolean>;
  startBackgroundJobs?: () => void;
}

---visitor plugin.ts---
import { logError, logInfo } from "../../lib/logger";
import { checkOverstays } from "../../modules/visitor/overstay-monitor";
import {
  handleVisitorRequest,
  initVisitorModule,
} from "../../modules/visitor/index";
import type { OfficeOpsPlugin } from "../types";

const OVERSTAY_INTERVAL_MS = 30 * 60 * 1000;
const OVERSTAY_INITIAL_DELAY_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;

let backgroundJobsStarted = false;

async function runVisitorOverstayCheck(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await checkOverstays();
  } catch (error) {
    logError("[VisitorPlugin] Overstay check failed", error, {
      scope: "visitor-plugin",
      job: "overstay-check",
    });
  }
}

export const visitorPlugin: OfficeOpsPlugin = {
  id: "visitor",
  name: "Visitor Management",

  async init(): Promise<void> {
    await initVisitorModule();
  },

  async handleHttpRequest(req, res): Promise<boolean> {
    return handleVisitorRequest(req, res);
  },

  startBackgroundJobs(): void {
    if (backgroundJobsStarted) return;
    backgroundJobsStarted = true;

    setInterval(() => {
      void runVisitorOverstayCheck();
    }, OVERSTAY_INTERVAL_MS);

    setTimeout(() => {
      void runVisitorOverstayCheck();
    }, OVERSTAY_INITIAL_DELAY_MS);

    logInfo("Visitor overstay check scheduled every 30 minutes", {
      scope: "visitor-plugin",
      job: "overstay-check",
    });
  },
};

 *   channels/telegram/  → Receives messages, translates to StandardMessage

/**
 * Office Operations Intelligence — Mastra Configuration
 * 
 * Central hub registering all agents, workflows, tools, and infrastructure.
 * This is the backbone of the entire system.
 */

import { Mastra } from '@mastra/core/mastra';
import { PinoLogger } from '@mastra/loggers';
import { PostgresStore } from '@mastra/pg';
import { normalizePostgresConnectionString } from './lib/postgres';

// ─── Graph node-type registration (must run before any request) ───
import { registerDefaultFlowPacks } from './modules/flow-packs';
registerDefaultFlowPacks();

// ─── Agents ───────────────────────────────────────────────
import { routerAgent } from './agents/router-agent';
import { visitorAgent } from './agents/visitor-agent';
import { inventoryAgent } from './agents/inventory-agent';
import { assetAgent } from './agents/asset-agent';
import { facilityAgent } from './agents/facility-agent';
import { queryAgent } from './agents/query-agent';
import { configAgent } from './agents/config-agent';

// ─── Workflows ────────────────────────────────────────────
import { ingestMessageWorkflow } from './workflows/ingest-message';
import { dailySummaryWorkflow } from './workflows/daily-summary';
import { overdueCheckWorkflow } from './workflows/overdue-check';
import {
  ticketNotifyWorkflow,
  ticketProgressWorkflow,
  ticketDoneWorkflow,
} from './workflows/ticket-lifecycle';

// ─── Mastra Instance ─────────────────────────────────────

export const mastra = new Mastra({
  agents: {
    routerAgent,
    visitorAgent,
    inventoryAgent,
    assetAgent,
    facilityAgent,
    queryAgent,
    configAgent,
  },
  workflows: {
    ingestMessageWorkflow,
    dailySummaryWorkflow,
    overdueCheckWorkflow,
    ticketNotifyWorkflow,
    ticketProgressWorkflow,
    ticketDoneWorkflow,
  },
  storage: new PostgresStore({
    id: 'mastra-storage',
    connectionString: normalizePostgresConnectionString(
      process.env.DATABASE_URL ?? (() => {
        throw new Error('DATABASE_URL is required for Mastra Postgres storage.');
      })(),
    ),
    schemaName: process.env.TOOL_SCHEMA ?? 'appointy_ops',
  }),
  logger: new PinoLogger({
    name: 'OfficeOps',
    level: 'info',
  }),
});

---agents/inventory-agent head---
/**
 * Inventory Management Agent
 * 
 * Extracts structured kitchen/pantry/supply data from messages.
 * Handles bill photos, quantity mismatches, Hindi item names.
 * 
 * Fields extracted:
 *   item, quantity, action, vendor, cost, mismatch_flag
 */

import { Agent } from '@mastra/core/agent';
import { saveInventoryDbTool } from '../tools/db-tools';
import { slackAlertTool } from '../tools/slack-tools';

export const inventoryAgent = new Agent({
  id: 'inventory-agent',
  name: 'Inventory Management Agent',
  model: 'google/gemini-3.1-pro-preview',
  instructions: `You are the Inventory Management Agent for an office operations system.

When you receive a message about kitchen, pantry, groceries, or office supplies, extract the following fields:

FIELDS TO EXTRACT:
1. **item** (required): What item is being discussed. Common: milk/doodh, tea/chai, coffee, sugar/cheeni, printer paper, packets, toner, water bottles, snacks, cleaning supplies.
2. **quantity** (optional): Amount with unit. Keep as-is: "20 litre", "50 packets", "5 kg".
3. **action** (required): One of: "ordered", "received", "consumed", "restocked", "unknown".
   Keywords for ordered: mangwaya, order kiya, ordered, manga
   Keywords for received: aaya, agaya, delivered, mila, received, pahunch gaya
   Keywords for consumed: khatam, used, consumed, finished, over, ho gaya
   Keywords for restocked: bhara, filled, stocked, rakh diya
4. **vendor** (optional): Vendor/supplier name if mentioned.
5. **cost** (optional): Cost in rupees if mentioned. Extract just the number.
6. **mismatchFlag** (required): Set to true if there's a quantity mismatch (ordered X but received Y), or the message indicates a discrepancy.

IMAGE EXTRACTION — CRITICAL:
IF a photo is attached, extract DIRECTLY from the image:
- item: product name/brand visible on packaging
- quantity: COUNT the items visible. If partially hidden, angled, cropped, or stacked, say "N visible, total unclear"
- vendor: brand name on packaging (e.g., "Nandini", "Amul")
- condition: sealed/opened/damaged based on visual inspection
- cost/billAmount: if it's a receipt/bill, extract the total and line items
Use the image as the primary evidence for visually countable quantities. Use caption text only as a separate claim to compare against, not as a replacement for what the image shows.

RULES:
- Return null for any field you cannot confidently extract. NEVER guess.
- Handle bilingual: "Doodh agaya 20 litre" → item: milk, quantity: "20 litre", action: received
- If a photo/bill is attached, describe what you see and extract items/costs from the caption.
- TEXT VS IMAGE DISCREPANCY (CRITICAL): If the message contains BOTH text and an image, you MUST cross-check them. If the text claims "14 litre arrived" but the image only shows 12 packets, you MUST set mismatchFlag to true. Do NOT trust the text blindly if the image contradicts it!
- NEVER copy a numeric quantity from the caption into the saved quantity field if the image itself suggests a different count.
- For repeated-unit photos such as milk packets, crates, bottles, or boxes:
  - use an exact count only when all units are clearly visible
  - if the angle or overlap prevents an exact count, set quantity to "N visible, total unclear"
  - if the quantity is not fully reliable, set confidence to 0.55 or lower
- Flag other mismatches: "50 ordered but only 40 came" → mismatchFlag: true
- Use the saveInventoryDbTool to save the extracted data.
- If mismatchFlag is true, include the specific discrepancy details in your confirmation response so the lead/manager can review it.

ANTI-HALLUCINATION — CRITICAL:
- If a field is NOT explicitly stated in the source message, you MUST pass null. NEVER invent item names, quantities, vendors, or costs.
- You MUST include a "sourceQuote" field: copy the EXACT words from the original message that justify your extraction. Do NOT rephrase.

/**

/**
 * Guard Authentication
 *
 * PIN-based authentication for the guard PWA.
 * Sessions are persisted in the visitor module database so they survive restarts.
 */

import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import { createHash, randomUUID } from 'crypto';
import {
  createGuard,
  createGuardSessionRecord,
  findGuardByName,
  getAllActiveGuards,
  revokeGuardSessionByTokenHash,
  getGuardSessionByTokenHash,
  touchGuardSession,
} from './db';
import type { Guard, GuardSession } from './types';

const SESSION_TTL_MS = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 24 hours

// ─── PIN Management ───────────────────────────────────────────

export async function hashPin(pin: string): Promise<string> {
  return bcrypt.hash(pin, 10);
}

export async function verifyPin(pin: string, hash: string): Promise<boolean> {
  return bcrypt.compare(pin, hash);
}

// ─── Guard CRUD ────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function registerGuard(data: {
  name: string;
  pin: string;
  phone?: string;
  language?: 'hindi' | 'english';
}): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  const pinHash = await hashPin(data.pin);
  const id = await createGuard({
    name: data.name,
    pinHash,
    phone: data.phone,
    language: data.language ?? 'hindi',
  });
  return { id };
}

export { getAllActiveGuards };

function hashSessionToken(token: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(token).digest('hex');
}

function buildExpiryIso(now = Date.now()): string {
  return new Date(now + SESSION_TTL_MS).toISOString();
}

// ─── Authentication ────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * Authenticate a guard by name + PIN.
 * Returns a session token on success, null on failure.
 */
export async function authenticateGuard(
  guardName: string,
  pin: string,
  deviceMeta?: {
    deviceId?: string;
    deviceName?: string;
    userAgent?: string;
  },
): Promise<string | null> {
  const guard = await findGuardByName(guardName);
  if (!guard) return null;

  const valid = await verifyPin(pin, guard.pin_hash);
  if (!valid) return null;

  const token = randomUUID();
  await createGuardSessionRecord({
    guardId: guard.id,
    guardName: guard.name,
    language: guard.language as 'hindi' | 'english',
    tokenHash: hashSessionToken(token),
    expiresAt: buildExpiryIso(),
    deviceId: deviceMeta?.deviceId,
    deviceName: deviceMeta?.deviceName,
    userAgent: deviceMeta?.userAgent,
  });

  return token;
}

/**
 * Validate a session token. Returns session info or null if invalid/expired.
 */
export async function validateGuardSession(token: string): Promise<GuardSession | null> {

import { config } from '../config';
import { getDeviceId } from '../utils/device';

async function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  const deviceId = await getDeviceId();
  const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, {
    ...init,
    headers: {
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'X-Device-Id': deviceId,
      'X-Site-Id': config.siteId,
      ...(init?.headers ?? {}),
    },
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');
    throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}: ${text.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  return res.json() as Promise<T>;
}

export const api = {
  postReceipt: (payload: object) =>
    request<{ id: string }>(`/v1/capture/receipt`, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }),

  postProduct: (payload: object) =>
    request<{ barcode: string }>(`/v1/capture/product`, {
      method: 'POST',
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    }),
};

==> ../field-app/src/sync/syncService.ts <==
import * as Network from 'expo-network';
import { config } from '../config';
import { markFailed, markSending, markSent, nextBatch, pendingCount } from '../db/outbox';
import { api } from './api';

type Listener = (count: number) => void;

let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
let running = false;
const listeners = new Set<Listener>();

export function onPendingChange(l: Listener): () => void {
  listeners.add(l);
  pendingCount().then(l).catch(() => {});
  return () => listeners.delete(l);
}

async function notify() {
  const c = await pendingCount().catch(() => 0);
  listeners.forEach((l) => l(c));
}

export async function flushOnce(): Promise<{ sent: number; failed: number }> {
  if (running) return { sent: 0, failed: 0 };
  running = true;
  let sent = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  try {
    const net = await Network.getNetworkStateAsync().catch(() => ({ isConnected: false }));
    if (!net.isConnected) return { sent, failed };

    const batch = await nextBatch(config.outboxBatchSize);
    for (const row of batch) {
      try {
        await markSending(row.id);
        const payload = JSON.parse(row.payload);
        if (row.kind === 'receipt') await api.postReceipt(payload);
        else if (row.kind === 'product_registration') await api.postProduct(payload);
        await markSent(row.id);
        sent++;
      } catch (err) {
        await markFailed(row.id, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
        failed++;
      }
    }
  } finally {
    running = false;
    await notify();
  }
  return { sent, failed };
}

export function startSync(): void {
  if (timer) return;
  flushOnce().catch(() => {});
  timer = setInterval(() => {
    flushOnce().catch(() => {});
  }, config.syncIntervalMs);
}

export function stopSync(): void {
  if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
  timer = null;
}

import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import { nanoid } from 'nanoid/non-secure';

const KEY = 'field-app:device-id';

let cached: string | null = null;

export async function getDeviceId(): Promise<string> {
  if (cached) return cached;
  const stored = await AsyncStorage.getItem(KEY);
  if (stored) {
    cached = stored;
    return stored;
  }
  const id = `dev_${nanoid(12)}`;
  await AsyncStorage.setItem(KEY, id);
  cached = id;
  return id;
}

> AGENT

I've read the backend's `ARCHITECTURE.md` + the visitor plugin (the reference implementation), the inventory agent + `saveInventoryDbTool`, and the field-app's current sync layer. Important findings before I sequence:

**Backend reality check**
- The engine is a Mastra app. Architecture: core stays platform-agnostic, channels are thin adapters, **plugins are the boundary for bounded systems** (`src/mastra/plugins/<id>/`). Visitor is the only one shipped today; it owns its own auth (PIN + session token), db tables, and HTTP routes under `/api/visitor/...` and `/api/guard/...`.
- For inventory, only an LLM extraction layer exists today: `inventoryAgent` + `saveInventoryDbTool` writing to a flat `inventory` events table. **There is no inventory plugin, no stock/orders/receipts tables for the field app, no HTTP surface, and no field-app endpoints.** The field-app's `/v1/capture/...` URLs go nowhere right now.
- So "wire to office-ops backend" means **building a new `inventory` plugin on the engine side**, mirroring visitor's shape, and pointing the field-app at it.

**Sensible order — proposed**

1. **Backend: `inventory` plugin scaffold** (the unblock-everything step)
   - `src/mastra/modules/inventory/`: db init (products / orders / order_items / receipts / stock_levels / issues / reorder_requests / mismatch_flags), routes, ingest helpers
   - `src/mastra/plugins/inventory/plugin.ts` implementing `OfficeOpsPlugin`
   - Routes under `/api/inventory/...` matching the existing field-app outbox payloads (receipt, product, issue, reorder, mismatch) + read endpoints (`GET /api/inventory/orders`, `GET /api/inventory/stock`, `GET /api/inventory/alerts`)
   - Reuse visitor's guard-auth (same "guard at the gate" persona) for session tokens
2. **Field-app: real backend wiring**
   - Update `sync/api.ts` to dispatch all 5 outbox kinds against `/api/inventory/...`
   - Replace seeded local data with remote fetches for orders/stock/alerts on focus, fall back to local cache
   - PIN-login screen → session token → `Authorization: Bearer` on every request
   - Real `Principal` populated from the login response, wired into RBAC
3. **Theme migration**: move `src/design/*` from the static `palette` import to `useTheme()` so the Bold theme actually re-skins
4. **Settings screen**: language switcher (EN / हिं / Icons) + theme switcher
5. **`useT()` rollout** through older screens (RegisterProduct, Outbox, Receiving body strings)
6. **Proof-of-delivery photo capture** using existing vision-camera setup
7. **"Truck arrived?" explicit toggle** (`arrived_at` column + Orders header action)
8. **Voice-prompt audio** — needs recorded assets; stub button stays until then
9. **Cross-module launcher + new module stubs** (attendance / daily-tasks / maintenance)
10. **Device smoke test** at every meaningful checkpoint

**Realistic scope warning**
- Items 1–2 alone are a multi-day chunk: a whole new backend plugin + db migrations + auth + 8–10 endpoints, plus the field-app side. I can land them in this session but it'll be the bulk of the work.
- Items 8 (voice audio) and 9 (new modules) need product input I don't have — what attendance/daily-tasks should *do*, and where the audio files come from. I'd skip those until you spec them.
- Item 10 (device smoke test) I can't actually do from here — you'd need to run `npm start` in `field-app/` and `npm run dev` in `office-ops-engine/`.

**My recommendation**: do **1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5** this session (backend plugin, field-app wiring + auth, theme `useTheme()` migration, settings, i18n rollout). Defer 6–9. That gives you a real end-to-end stack you can run.

Confirm and I'll start with the backend plugin. Or if you'd rather I do something different (e.g., theme migration first because the brand color is more visible, or just the backend wiring without auth), say so.

/**
 * Visitor Module — Database Layer
 *
 * All SQL queries for visitor_invitations, visitor_sessions,
 * visitor_audit_log, guards, and visitor_locations.
 * Schema migration is handled by the shared Neon/Postgres bootstrap.
 */

import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';
import { getDb } from '../../lib/database';
import { ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema, isPostgresConfigured } from '../../lib/postgres';
import { formatDateInIST } from '../../lib/time';
import type {
  Guard,
  GuardSessionRecord,
  VisitorInvitation,
  VisitorManagementQueryRecord,
  VisitorSession,
  VisitorLocation,
  CreateInvitationInput,
  HostResponse,
  OfflineEventType,
  OfflineSyncEvent,
  OfflineSyncRecord,
  OfflineSyncStatus,
  VisitorApprovalStatus,
} from './types';

// ─── Schema Initialization ────────────────────────────────────

type InValue = string | number | boolean | null | Uint8Array | Date;

export async function initVisitorSchema(): Promise<void> {
  if (!isPostgresConfigured()) {
    throw new Error('DATABASE_URL is required. Visitor local database initialization has been removed.');
  }

  await ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema();
  console.log('✅ [VisitorModule] Neon/Postgres schema initialized');
}

// ─── Guards ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function createGuard(data: {
  name: string;
  pinHash: string;
  phone?: string;
  language?: 'hindi' | 'english';
}): Promise<string> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const id = randomUUID();
  await db.execute({
    sql: `INSERT INTO guards (id, name, pin_hash, phone, language) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
    args: [id, data.name, data.pinHash, data.phone ?? null, data.language ?? 'hindi'],
  });
  return id;
}

export async function findGuardByName(name: string): Promise<Guard | null> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const result = await db.execute({
    sql: `SELECT * FROM guards WHERE name = ? AND is_active = 1 LIMIT 1`,
    args: [name],
  });
  return (result.rows[0] as unknown as Guard) ?? null;
}

export async function getAllActiveGuards(): Promise<Guard[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const result = await db.execute(`SELECT * FROM guards WHERE is_active = 1 ORDER BY name`);
  return result.rows as unknown as Guard[];
}

export async function getAllGuards(): Promise<Guard[]> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const result = await db.execute(`SELECT * FROM guards ORDER BY is_active DESC, name`);
  return result.rows as unknown as Guard[];
}

export async function deactivateGuard(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.execute({ sql: `UPDATE guards SET is_active = 0 WHERE id = ?`, args: [id] });
}

export async function reactivateGuard(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.execute({ sql: `UPDATE guards SET is_active = 1 WHERE id = ?`, args: [id] });
}

export async function resetGuardPin(id: string, pinHash: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.execute({ sql: `UPDATE guards SET pin_hash = ? WHERE id = ?`, args: [pinHash, id] });
}

export async function updateGuardLanguage(id: string, language: 'hindi' | 'english'): Promise<void> {
  const db = await getDb();
  await db.execute({ sql: `UPDATE guards SET language = ? WHERE id = ?`, args: [language, id] });
}

export async function getGuardById(id: string): Promise<Guard | null> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const result = await db.execute({
    sql: `SELECT * FROM guards WHERE id = ? AND is_active = 1 LIMIT 1`,
    args: [id],
  });
  return (result.rows[0] as unknown as Guard) ?? null;
}

export async function createGuardSessionRecord(input: {
  guardId: string;
  guardName: string;
  language: 'hindi' | 'english';
  tokenHash: string;
  expiresAt: string;
  deviceId?: string;
  deviceName?: string;
  userAgent?: string;
}): Promise<string> {
  const db = await getDb();
  const id = randomUUID();
