1	import { drizzle, type NodePgDatabase } from "drizzle-orm/node-postgres";
2	import { Pool } from "pg";
3	import * as schema from "../db/schema";
4	import { officeOpsSchemaName, officeOpsSchemaSql, quoteIdentifier } from "./postgres-schema";
5	
6	let pool: Pool | null = null;
7	let db: NodePgDatabase<typeof schema> | null = null;
8	let schemaReady: Promise<void> | null = null;
9	
10	export function normalizePostgresConnectionString(connectionString: string): string {
11	  const url = new URL(connectionString);
12	  const sslMode = url.searchParams.get("sslmode");
13	  if (!sslMode && url.hostname.includes("neon.tech")) {
14	    url.searchParams.set("sslmode", "verify-full");
15	    return url.toString();
16	  }
17	  if (sslMode && ["prefer", "require", "verify-ca"].includes(sslMode)) {
18	    url.searchParams.set("sslmode", "verify-full");
19	    return url.toString();
20	  }
21	  return connectionString;
22	}
23	
24	export function isPostgresConfigured(): boolean {
25	  return Boolean(process.env.DATABASE_URL);
26	}
27	
28	export function getPostgresDb(): NodePgDatabase<typeof schema> {
29	  if (db) return db;
30	
31	  db = drizzle(getPostgresPool(), { schema });
32	  return db;
33	}
34	
35	export function getPostgresPool(): Pool {
36	  if (pool) return pool;
37	
38	  const connectionString = process.env.DATABASE_URL;
39	  if (!connectionString) {
40	    throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is not configured");
41	  }
42	
43	  pool = new Pool({
44	    connectionString: normalizePostgresConnectionString(connectionString),
45	    keepAlive: true,
46	    idleTimeoutMillis: 10000,
47	    connectionTimeoutMillis: 5000,
48	  });
49	
50	  pool.on("connect", (client) => {
51	    client.query(`SET search_path TO ${officeOpsSchemaName()},public`).catch((err) => {
52	      console.error("[postgres] Error setting search_path:", err.message);
53	    });
54	  });
55	
56	  // Prevent unhandled 'error' events from crashing the process when Neon
57	  // terminates idle connections — pg-pool emits on the pool, not the caller.
58	  pool.on("error", (err) => {
59	    console.error("[postgres] Idle client error (connection terminated):", err.message);
60	  });
61	
62	  return pool;
63	}
64	
65	export function getOptionalPostgresDb(): NodePgDatabase<typeof schema> | null {
66	  if (!isPostgresConfigured()) return null;
67	  return getPostgresDb();
68	}
69	
70	export async function closePostgresDb(): Promise<void> {
71	  await pool?.end();
72	  pool = null;
73	  db = null;
74	  schemaReady = null;
75	}
76	
77	export async function ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema(): Promise<void> {
78	  if (!isPostgresConfigured()) {
79	    throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is not configured");
80	  }

1	export function quoteIdentifier(value: string): string {
2	  if (!/^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/.test(value)) {
3	    throw new Error(`Invalid PostgreSQL identifier: ${value}`);
4	  }
5	  return `"${value}"`;
6	}
7

14	  return `

17	    SET search_path TO ${schema}, public;

82	export async function getDb(): Promise<Client> {
83	  if (_db) return _db;
84	
85	  if (!isPostgresConfigured()) {
86	    throw new Error("DATABASE_URL is required. Office Ops now uses Neon/Postgres only.");
87	  }
88	
89	  await ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema();
90	  _db = createPostgresCompatClient();
91	  return _db;
92	}
93	
94	export async function resetDatabase(): Promise<{
95	  clearedTables: readonly string[];
96	}> {
97	  const db = await getDb();
98	  await db.batch(
99	    RESET_TABLES_IN_ORDER.map((table) => `DELETE FROM ${table}`),
100	    "write",
101	  );
102	
103	  return {
104	    clearedTables: RESET_TABLES_IN_ORDER,
105	  };
106	}
107	
108	function createPostgresCompatClient(): Client {
109	  return {
110	    async execute(statement: SqlStatement): Promise<SqlResult> {
111	      const normalized = normalizePostgresStatement(statement);
112	      if (!normalized.sql.trim()) return { rows: [], rowsAffected: 0 };
113	      const result = await getPostgresPool().query(normalized.sql, normalized.args);
114	      return {
115	        rows: result.rows,
116	        rowsAffected: result.rowCount ?? 0,
117	      };
118	    },
119	    async executeMultiple(sql: string): Promise<void> {
120	      for (const statement of splitSqlStatements(sql)) {
121	        const normalized = normalizePostgresStatement(statement);
122	        if (normalized.sql.trim()) {
123	          await getPostgresPool().query(normalized.sql, normalized.args);
124	        }
125	      }
126	    },
127	    async batch(statements: readonly SqlStatement[]): Promise<SqlResult[]> {
128	      const client = await getPostgresPool().connect();
129	      try {
130	        await client.query("BEGIN");
131	        const results: SqlResult[] = [];
132	        for (const statement of statements) {
133	          const normalized = normalizePostgresStatement(statement);
134	          if (!normalized.sql.trim()) {
135	            results.push({ rows: [], rowsAffected: 0 });
136	            continue;
137	          }
138	          const result = await client.query(normalized.sql, normalized.args);
139	          results.push({ rows: result.rows, rowsAffected: result.rowCount ?? 0 });
140	        }
141	        await client.query("COMMIT");
142	        return results;
143	      } catch (error) {
144	        await client.query("ROLLBACK");
145	        throw error;
146	      } finally {
147	        client.release();
148	      }
149	    },
150	  };
151	}
152	
153	function normalizePostgresStatement(statement: SqlStatement): { sql: string; args: SqlArgs } {
154	  const rawSql = typeof statement === "string" ? statement : statement.sql;
155	  const args = typeof statement === "string" ? [] : statement.args ?? [];
156	  let sql = rawSql.trim();
157	
158	  if (!sql) {
159	    return { sql: "", args };
160	  }
161	
162	  sql = sql
163	    .replace(/INSERT\s+OR\s+REPLACE\s+INTO\s+staff_channels\s*\(([^)]+)\)\s*VALUES\s*\(([^)]+)\)/i,
164	      "INSERT INTO staff_channels ($1) VALUES ($2) ON CONFLICT (channel, channel_uid) DO UPDATE SET staff_id = EXCLUDED.staff_id, channel_uname = EXCLUDED.channel_uname, created_at = EXCLUDED.created_at")
```

177	
178	function replaceQuestionPlaceholders(sql: string): string {
179	  let index = 0;
180	  let output = "";
181	  let inSingleQuote = false;
182	  for (let i = 0; i < sql.length; i++) {
183	    const char = sql[i];
184	    const next = sql[i + 1];
185	    if (char === "'" && next === "'") {
186	      output += char + next;
187	      i++;
188	      continue;
189	    }
190	    if (char === "'") {
191	      inSingleQuote = !inSingleQuote;
192	      output += char;
193	      continue;
194	    }
195	    if (char === "?" && !inSingleQuote) {
196	      index++;
197	      output += `$${index}`;
198	      continue;
199	    }
200	    output += char;
201	  }
202	  return output;
203	}
204	
205	function splitSqlStatements(sql: string): string[] {
206	  return sql
207	    .split(";")
208	    .map((statement) => statement.trim())
209	    .filter(Boolean);
210	}

155	function json(res: ServerResponse, status: number, data: unknown): void {
156	  const body = JSON.stringify(data);
157	  res.writeHead(status, {
158	    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
159	    'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
160	    'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET,POST,PUT,PATCH,DELETE,OPTIONS',
161	    'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type,Accept,Authorization,x-admin-secret,x-actor-name,x-actor-email,x-actor-role,x-guard-token,x-invited-by,x-email-template-key,x-pass-template-key',
162	  });
163	  res.end(body);
164	}
165	
166	
167	async function getActiveBroadcastForInvitation(invitationId: string): Promise<{
168	  id: string;
169	  message: string;
170	  createdAt: string;
171	} | null> {
172	  const db = getOptionalPostgresDb();
173	  if (!db) return null;
174	
175	  const { getSessionByInvitation } = await import("./db");
176	  const session = await getSessionByInvitation(invitationId);
177	  if (!session?.checkin_time) return null;
178	
179	  try {
180	    const rows = await db
181	      .select()
182	      .from(visitorBroadcasts)
183	      .where(eq(visitorBroadcasts.status, "active"))
184	      .orderBy(desc(visitorBroadcasts.createdAt))
185	      .limit(10);
186	
187	    const checkinAt = new Date(session.checkin_time).getTime();
188	    const now = Date.now();
189	    const active = rows.find((row) => {
190	      const createdAt = row.createdAt ? new Date(row.createdAt).getTime() : 0;
191	      const expiresAt = row.expiresAt ? new Date(row.expiresAt).getTime() : null;
192	      return createdAt >= checkinAt && (expiresAt == null || expiresAt > now);
193	    });
194	
195	    return active ? {
196	      id: active.id,
197	      message: active.message,
198	      createdAt: active.createdAt?.toISOString?.() ?? String(active.createdAt),
199	    } : null;
200	  } catch {
201	    return null;
202	  }
203	}
204	
205	function corsPreflight(res: ServerResponse): void {
206	  res.writeHead(204, {
207	    'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*',
208	    'Access-Control-Allow-Methods': 'GET,POST,PUT,PATCH,DELETE,OPTIONS',
209	    'Access-Control-Allow-Headers': 'Content-Type,Accept,Authorization,x-admin-secret,x-actor-name,x-actor-email,x-actor-role,x-guard-token,x-invited-by,x-email-template-key,x-pass-template-key',
210	    'Access-Control-Max-Age': '86400',
211	  });
212	  res.end();
213	}
214	
215	async function readBody(req: IncomingMessage): Promise<string> {
216	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
217	    let data = '';
218	    req.on('data', (chunk) => { data += chunk; });
219	    req.on('end', () => resolve(data));
220	    req.on('error', reject);
221	  });
222	}
223	
224	async function readBodyJson(req: IncomingMessage): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {
225	  const raw = await readBody(req);
226	  try { return JSON.parse(raw); } catch { return {}; }
227	}
228	
229	async function guardAuth(req: IncomingMessage): Promise<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof validateGuardSession>>> {
230	  const token = req.headers['x-guard-token'] as string | undefined
231	    ?? parseUrl(req.url ?? '', true).query['token'] as string | undefined;
232	  if (!token) return null;
233	  return validateGuardSession(token);
234	}

// ─── Route Matcher ────────────────────────────────────────────

export async function handleVisitorRequest(
  req: IncomingMessage,
  res: ServerResponse,
): Promise<boolean> {
  const { pathname, query } = parseUrl(req.url ?? '', true);
  const method = req.method?.toUpperCase() ?? 'GET';

  if (!pathname) return false;

  if (method === 'OPTIONS') {
    corsPreflight(res);
    return true;
  }

  // ── Visitor phone OTP (dormant; gated by VISITOR_OTP_ENABLED) ──
  // Wired but not used by the registration flow. Flip the env flag and
  // call from WalkInRegisterPage once an SMS provider is configured.

  if (pathname === '/api/visitor/otp/request' && method === 'POST') {
    const { isVisitorOtpEnabled, sendVisitorOtp } = await import('./visitor-otp');
    if (!isVisitorOtpEnabled()) {
      json(res, 501, { error: 'Visitor OTP is not enabled.' });
      return true;
    }
    const body = await readBodyJson(req);
    const phone = validateVisitorPhone(normalizeBodyString(body.phone));
    if (!phone) {
      json(res, 400, { error: 'Valid phone number required.' });
```

/**
 * System Plugin Registry
 *
 * Plugin modules own their bounded business logic while the core
 * coordinates shared infrastructure. This keeps subsystems like
 * visitor management attachable without hard-wiring them into core.
 */

import { registerPlugin } from "./registry";
import { visitorPlugin } from "./visitor";

registerPlugin(visitorPlugin);

export {
  getAllPlugins,
  getPlugin,
  handlePluginHttpRequest,
  initPlugins,
  registerPlugin,
  startPluginBackgroundJobs,
} from "./registry";
export type { OfficeOpsPlugin } from "./types";

These tables back the field-app gate flow:\n    -- a guard scans, the device queues a write, the server materializes\n    -- catalog/stock/order state. Every row is site-scoped.\n\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_products (\n      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,\n      site_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      name TEXT NOT NULL,\n      category TEXT,\n      unit TEXT,\n      registered_by TEXT,\n      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n      updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n      PRIMARY KEY (site_id, barcode)\n    );\n\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_orders (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      site_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      vendor TEXT,\n      expected_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n      arrived_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',\n      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n      updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n    );\n\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_order_items (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      order_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES inventory_orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n      barcode TEXT,\n      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n      expected_qty NUMERIC NOT NULL,\n      received_qty NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0\n    );\n\n    -- Receipts are the source of truth for what was scanned at the gate.\n    -- Idempotent on `id` so the field-app outbox can safely retry.\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_receipts (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      site_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      order_id TEXT REFERENCES inventory_orders(id),\n      order_item_id TEXT REFERENCES inventory_order_items(id),\n      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,\n      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n      qty NUMERIC NOT NULL,\n      flagged BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false,\n      device_id TEXT,\n      guard_id TEXT REFERENCES guards(id),\n      scanned_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n    );\n\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_stock (\n      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,\n      site_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      name TEXT NOT NULL,\n      category TEXT,\n      unit TEXT,\n      on_hand NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n      threshold NUMERIC NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n      updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n      PRIMARY KEY (site_id, barcode)\n    );\n\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_issues (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      site_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,\n      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n      qty NUMERIC NOT NULL,\n      reason TEXT,\n      taken_by TEXT,\n      device_id TEXT,\n      guard_id TEXT REFERENCES guards(id),\n      issued_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n    );\n\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_reorder_requests (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      site_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,\n      product_name TEXT NOT NULL,\n      on_hand NUMERIC,\n      threshold NUMERIC,\n      requested_by TEXT,\n      device_id TEXT,\n      guard_id TEXT REFERENCES guards(id),\n      status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open',\n      requested_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n    );\n\n    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS inventory_mismatch_flags (\n      id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n      site_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n      receipt_id TEXT REFERENCES inventory_receipts(id),\n      order_item_id TEXT REFERENCES inventory_order_items(id),\n      barcode TEXT NOT NULL,\n      expected NUMERIC,\n      received_total NUMERIC,\n      note TEXT,\n      device_id TEXT,\n      guard_id TEXT REFERENCES guards(id),\n      flagged_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n      created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n    );\n\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inv_orders_site_status ON inventory_orders(site_id, status);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inv_order_items_order ON inventory_order_items(order_id);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inv_order_items_barcode ON inventory_order_items(barcode);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inv_receipts_site ON inventory_receipts(site_id, scanned_at DESC);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inv_stock_low ON inventory_stock(site_id) WHERE on_hand <= threshold;\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inv_issues_site ON inventory_issues(site_id, issued_at DESC);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inv_reorder_open ON inventory_reorder_requests(site_id, status);\n    CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_inv_mismatch_site ON inventory_mismatch_flags(site_id, flagged_at DESC);\n  `;\n}"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/inventory/db.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Inventory Plugin — Database Layer\n *\n * SQL surface backing the field-app gate-entry flow. Distinct from the\n * legacy top-level `inventory` table, which stores LLM extractions from\n * chat messages — these tables persist the structured capture stream\n * (products, orders, receipts, stock, issues, reorder requests,\n * mismatch flags).\n *\n * Schema migration runs from `lib/postgres-schema.ts` via\n * `ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema()`, the same boot path the visitor\n * module uses. This file owns the CRUD only.\n */\n\nimport { getDb } from \"../../lib/database\";\nimport { ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema } from \"../../lib/postgres\";\n\nexport type InventoryProduct = {\n  barcode: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string | null;\n  unit: string | null;\n  registered_by: string | null;\n  created_at: string;\n  updated_at: string;\n};\n\nexport type InventoryOrder = {\n  id: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  vendor: string | null;\n  expected_at: string | null;\n  arrived_at: string | null;\n  status: string;\n  created_at: string;\n  updated_at: string;\n};\n\nexport type InventoryOrderItem = {\n  id: string;\n  order_id: string;\n  barcode: string | null;\n  product_name: string;\n  expected_qty: number;\n  received_qty: number;\n};\n\nexport type InventoryStockRow = {\n  barcode: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string | null;\n  unit: string | null;\n  on_hand: number;\n  threshold: number;\n  updated_at: string;\n};\n\nexport type InventoryReceipt = {\n  id: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  order_id: string | null;\n  order_item_id: string | null;\n  barcode: string;\n  product_name: string;\n  qty: number;\n  flagged: boolean;\n  device_id: string | null;\n  guard_id: string | null;\n  scanned_at: string;\n};\n\nexport async function initInventorySchema(): Promise<void> {\n  await ensureOfficeOpsPostgresSchema();\n}\n\n// ─── Products ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport async function upsertProduct(input: {\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  name: string;\n  category?: string | null;\n  unit?: string | null;\n  registeredBy?: string | null;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_products (barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, registered_by)\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET\n        name = EXCLUDED.name,\n        category = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.category, inventory_products.category),\n        unit = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.unit, inventory_products.unit),\n        updated_at = now()\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.barcode,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.name,\n      input.category ?? null,\n      input.unit ?? null,\n      input.registeredBy ?? null,\n    ],\n  });\n}\n\nexport async function findProduct(\n  siteId: string,\n  barcode: string,\n): Promise<InventoryProduct | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_products WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? LIMIT 1`,\n    args: [siteId, barcode],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryProduct | undefined) ?? null;\n}\n\n// ─── Orders ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport async function listOpenOrders(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryOrder[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_orders WHERE site_id = ? AND status = 'open' ORDER BY expected_at ASC NULLS LAST`,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as InventoryOrder[];\n}\n\nexport async function getOrderItems(orderId: string): Promise<InventoryOrderItem[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_order_items WHERE order_id = ?`,\n    args: [orderId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as InventoryOrderItem[];\n}\n\nexport async function findOpenItemByBarcode(\n  siteId: string,\n  barcode: string,\n): Promise<InventoryOrderItem | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT oi.* FROM inventory_order_items oi\n      JOIN inventory_orders o ON o.id = oi.order_id\n      WHERE oi.barcode = ? AND o.site_id = ? AND o.status = 'open'\n      ORDER BY o.expected_at ASC NULLS LAST\n      LIMIT 1\n    `,\n    args: [barcode, siteId],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryOrderItem | undefined) ?? null;\n}\n\nexport async function addReceivedQty(itemId: string, delta: number): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `UPDATE inventory_order_items SET received_qty = received_qty + ? WHERE id = ?`,\n    args: [delta, itemId],\n  });\n}\n\nexport async function markOrderArrived(orderId: string, when: string): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `UPDATE inventory_orders SET arrived_at = ?, updated_at = now() WHERE id = ? AND arrived_at IS NULL`,\n    args: [when, orderId],\n  });\n}\n\n// ─── Receipts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * Idempotent on `id`. Field-app outbox retries land here without\n * double-counting because we ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING and report whether\n * the row was actually inserted.\n */\nexport async function insertReceipt(input: {\n  id: string;\n  siteId: string;\n  orderId?: string | null;\n  orderItemId?: string | null;\n  barcode: string;\n  productName: string;\n  qty: number;\n  flagged?: boolean;\n  deviceId?: string | null;\n  guardId?: string | null;\n  scannedAt: string;\n}): Promise<{ inserted: boolean }> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_receipts (\n        id, site_id, order_id, order_item_id, barcode, product_name,\n        qty, flagged, device_id, guard_id, scanned_at\n      )\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.id,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.orderId ?? null,\n      input.orderItemId ?? null,\n      input.barcode,\n      input.productName,\n      input.qty,\n      input.flagged ?? false,\n      input.deviceId ?? null,\n      input.guardId ?? null,\n      input.scannedAt,\n    ],\n  });\n  return { inserted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };\n}\n\n// ─── Stock ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport async function listStock(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_stock WHERE site_id = ? ORDER BY name`,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as InventoryStockRow[];\n}\n\nexport async function listLowOrOut(siteId: string): Promise<InventoryStockRow[]> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      SELECT * FROM inventory_stock\n      WHERE site_id = ? AND on_hand <= threshold\n      ORDER BY on_hand ASC, name ASC\n    `,\n    args: [siteId],\n  });\n  return result.rows as InventoryStockRow[];\n}\n\nexport async function findStock(\n  siteId: string,\n  barcode: string,\n): Promise<InventoryStockRow | null> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `SELECT * FROM inventory_stock WHERE site_id = ? AND barcode = ? LIMIT 1`,\n    args: [siteId, barcode],\n  });\n  return (result.rows[0] as InventoryStockRow | undefined) ?? null;\n}\n\nexport async function upsertStock(input: {\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  name: string;\n  category?: string | null;\n  unit?: string | null;\n  onHand?: number;\n  threshold?: number;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_stock (\n        barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold\n      )\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET\n        name = EXCLUDED.name,\n        category = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.category, inventory_stock.category),\n        unit = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.unit, inventory_stock.unit),\n        updated_at = now()\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.barcode,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.name,\n      input.category ?? null,\n      input.unit ?? null,\n      input.onHand ?? 0,\n      input.threshold ?? 0,\n    ],\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Apply a quantity delta to the on-hand level. Creates the row from the\n * receipt's product info if it doesn't yet exist, so the field-app's\n * Stock screen surfaces new SKUs as soon as they're scanned.\n */\nexport async function adjustOnHand(input: {\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  delta: number;\n  fallbackName?: string;\n  fallbackCategory?: string | null;\n  fallbackUnit?: string | null;\n}): Promise<void> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_stock (barcode, site_id, name, category, unit, on_hand, threshold)\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, GREATEST(?, 0), 0)\n      ON CONFLICT (site_id, barcode) DO UPDATE SET\n        on_hand = GREATEST(inventory_stock.on_hand + EXCLUDED.on_hand, 0),\n        updated_at = now()\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.barcode,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.fallbackName ?? \"Unknown\",\n      input.fallbackCategory ?? null,\n      input.fallbackUnit ?? null,\n      input.delta,\n    ],\n  });\n}\n\n// ─── Issues / Reorders / Mismatches ──────────────────────────────\n\nexport async function insertIssue(input: {\n  id: string;\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  productName: string;\n  qty: number;\n  reason?: string | null;\n  takenBy?: string | null;\n  deviceId?: string | null;\n  guardId?: string | null;\n  issuedAt: string;\n}): Promise<{ inserted: boolean }> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_issues (\n        id, site_id, barcode, product_name, qty, reason, taken_by,\n        device_id, guard_id, issued_at\n      )\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.id,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.barcode,\n      input.productName,\n      input.qty,\n      input.reason ?? null,\n      input.takenBy ?? null,\n      input.deviceId ?? null,\n      input.guardId ?? null,\n      input.issuedAt,\n    ],\n  });\n  return { inserted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };\n}\n\nexport async function insertReorderRequest(input: {\n  id: string;\n  siteId: string;\n  barcode: string;\n  productName: string;\n  onHand?: number | null;\n  threshold?: number | null;\n  requestedBy?: string | null;\n  deviceId?: string | null;\n  guardId?: string | null;\n  requestedAt: string;\n}): Promise<{ inserted: boolean }> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_reorder_requests (\n        id, site_id, barcode, product_name, on_hand, threshold,\n        requested_by, device_id, guard_id, status, requested_at\n      )\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'open', ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.id,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.barcode,\n      input.productName,\n      input.onHand ?? null,\n      input.threshold ?? null,\n      input.requestedBy ?? null,\n      input.deviceId ?? null,\n      input.guardId ?? null,\n      input.requestedAt,\n    ],\n  });\n  return { inserted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };\n}\n\nexport async function insertMismatchFlag(input: {\n  id: string;\n  siteId: string;\n  receiptId?: string | null;\n  orderItemId?: string | null;\n  barcode: string;\n  expected?: number | null;\n  receivedTotal?: number | null;\n  note?: string | null;\n  deviceId?: string | null;\n  guardId?: string | null;\n  flaggedAt: string;\n}): Promise<{ inserted: boolean }> {\n  const db = await getDb();\n  const result = await db.execute({\n    sql: `\n      INSERT INTO inventory_mismatch_flags (\n        id, site_id, receipt_id, order_item_id, barcode, expected,\n        received_total, note, device_id, guard_id, flagged_at\n      )\n      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\n      ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING\n    `,\n    args: [\n      input.id,\n      input.siteId,\n      input.receiptId ?? null,\n      input.orderItemId ?? null,\n      input.barcode,\n      input.expected ?? null,\n      input.receivedTotal ?? null,\n      input.note ?? null,\n      input.deviceId ?? null,\n      input.guardId ?? null,\n      input.flaggedAt,\n    ],\n  });\n  return { inserted: (result.rowsAffected ?? 0) > 0 };\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/office-ops-engine/src/mastra/modules/inventory/routes.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Inventory Plugin — HTTP Routes\n *\n * Field-app facing surface under /api/inventory/*.\n *\n * Writes (idempotent on `id`):\n *   POST /api/inventory/receipts          — gate capture\n *   POST /api/inventory/products          — catalog upsert (register barcode)\n *   POST /api/inventory/issues            — consumption\n *   POST /api/inventory/reorder-requests  — low-stock reorder ask\n *   POST /api/inventory/mismatch-flags    — receipt mismatch report\n *\n * Reads (guard-authenticated):\n *   GET  /api/inventory/orders            — open orders + items\n *   GET  /api/inventory/stock             — all stock rows\n *   GET  /api/inventory/alerts            — low + out of stock\n *   GET  /api/inventory/catalog/:barcode  — product lookup\n *\n * Auth: reuses the visitor module's guard session token. The field-app\n * obtains it via POST /api/guard/login (visitor routes) and sends it as\n * the `x-guard-token` header. Site selection is done with the\n * `x-site-id` header (defaults to the guard's session site when added).\n */\n\nimport type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from \"http\";\nimport { parse as parseUrl } from \"url\";\n\nimport { logError } from \"../../lib/logger\";\nimport { validateGuardSession } from \"../visitor/guard-auth\";\nimport {\n  addReceivedQty,\n  adjustOnHand,\n  findOpenItemByBarcode,\n  findProduct,\n  getOrderItems,\n  insertIssue,\n  insertMismatchFlag,\n  insertReceipt,\n  insertReorderRequest,\n  listLowOrOut,\n  listOpenOrders,\n  listStock,\n  upsertProduct,\n  upsertStock,\n} from \"./db\";\n\n// ─── Helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst CORS_HEADERS = {\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Origin\": \"*\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Methods\": \"GET,POST,PUT,PATCH,DELETE,OPTIONS\",\n  \"Access-Control-Allow-Headers\":\n    \"Content-Type,Accept,Authorization,x-guard-token,x-site-id,x-device-id\",\n};\n\nfunction json(res: ServerResponse, status: number, data: unknown): void {\n  res.writeHead(status, { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\", ...CORS_HEADERS });\n  res.end(JSON.stringify(data));\n}\n\nfunction corsPreflight(res: ServerResponse): void {\n  res.writeHead(204, { ...CORS_HEADERS, \"Access-Control-Max-Age\": \"86400\" });\n  res.end();\n}\n\nasync function readBodyJson(req: IncomingMessage): Promise<Record<string, unknown>> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    let raw = \"\";\n    req.on(\"data\", (chunk) => {\n      raw += chunk;\n    });\n    req.on(\"end\", () => {\n      try {\n        resolve(raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {});\n      } catch {\n        resolve({});\n      }\n    });\n    req.on(\"error\", reject);\n  });\n}\n\nfunction header(req: IncomingMessage, name: string): string | undefined {\n  const v = req.headers[name];\n  return Array.isArray(v) ? v[0] : v;\n}\n\ntype AuthedRequest = {\n  guardId: string;\n  guardName: string;\n  siteId: string;\n  deviceId: string | null;\n};\n\nasync function authenticate(\n  req: IncomingMessage,\n  res: ServerResponse,\n): Promise<AuthedRequest | null> {\n  const token =\n    header(req, \"x-guard-token\") ??\n    (parseUrl(req.url ?? \"\", true).query[\"token\"] as string | undefined);\n  if (!token) {\n    json(res, 401, { error: \"Missing x-guard-token\" });\n    return null;\n  }\n  const session = await validateGuardSession(token);\n  if (!session) {\n    json(res, 401, { error: \"Invalid or expired session\" });\n    return null;\n  }\n  const siteId = header(req, \"x-site-id\");\n  if (!siteId) {\n    json(res, 400, { error: \"Missing x-site-id\" });\n    return null;\n  }\n  return {\n    guardId: session.guardId,\n    guardName: session.guardName,\n    siteId,\n    deviceId: header(req, \"x-device-id\") ?? null,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction asString(v: unknown): string | null {\n  if (typeof v !== \"string\") return null;\n  const t = v.trim();\n  return t.length === 0 ? null : t;\n}\n\nfunction asNumber(v: unknown): number | null {\n  if (typeof v === \"number\" && Number.isFinite(v)) return v;\n  if (typeof v === \"string\") {\n    const n = Number(v);\n    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nfunction asBool(v: unknown): boolean {\n  return v === true || v === \"true\" || v === 1;\n}\n\nfunction isoOrNow(v: unknown): string {\n  if (typeof v === \"number\") return new Date(v).toISOString();\n  if (typeof v === \"string\") {\n    const t = Date.parse(v);\n    if (!Number.isNaN(t)) return new Date(t).toISOString();\n  }\n  return new Date().toISOString();\n}\n\n// ─── Route Matcher ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport async function handleInventoryRequest(\n  req: IncomingMessage,\n  res: ServerResponse,\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  const { pathname } = parseUrl(req.url ?? \"\", true);\n  const method = req.method?.toUpperCase() ?? \"GET\";\n\n  if (!pathname || !pathname.startsWith(\"/api/inventory/\")) return false;\n\n  if (method === \"OPTIONS\") {\n    corsPreflight(res);\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    return await dispatch(req, res, pathname, method);\n  } catch (error) {\n    logError(\"[InventoryRoutes] Unhandled error\", error, {\n      scope: \"inventory-plugin\",\n      pathname,\n      method,\n    });\n    json(res, 500, { error: \"Internal error\" });\n    return true;\n  }\n}\n\nasync function dispatch(\n  req: IncomingMessage,\n  res: ServerResponse,\n  pathname: string,\n  method: string,\n): Promise<boolean> {\n  // ── Writes ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  if (pathname === \"/api/inventory/receipts\" && method === \"POST\") {\n    const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n    if (!auth) return true;\n    const body = await readBodyJson(req);\n\n    const id = asString(body.id);\n    const barcode = asString(body.barcode);\n    const qty = asNumber(body.qty);\n    if (!id || !barcode || qty === null) {\n      json(res, 400, { error: \"id, barcode, qty are required\" });\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    const productName = asString(body.product_name) ?? \"Unknown\";\n    const orderId = asString(body.order_id);\n    const orderItemId = asString(body.order_item_id);\n    const flagged = asBool(body.flagged);\n    const scannedAt = isoOrNow(body.scanned_at);\n\n    const result = await insertReceipt({\n      id,\n      siteId: auth.siteId,\n      orderId,\n      orderItemId,\n      barcode,\n      productName,\n      qty,\n      flagged,\n      deviceId: auth.deviceId,\n      guardId: auth.guardId,\n      scannedAt,\n    });\n\n    // Only mutate downstream state on the first successful insert.\n    // Retries land here too (id is idempotent) but must be no-ops.\n    if (result.inserted) {\n      if (orderItemId) {\n        await addReceivedQty(orderItemId, qty);\n      }\n      const existing = await findProduct(auth.siteId, barcode);\n      await adjustOnHand({\n        siteId: auth.siteId,\n        barcode,\n        delta: qty,\n        fallbackName: productName,\n        fallbackCategory: existing?.category ?? null,\n        fallbackUnit: existing?.unit ?? null,\n      });\n    }\n\n    json(res, 200, { id, inserted: result.inserted });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  if (pathname === \"/api/inventory/products\" && method === \"POST\") {\n    const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n    if (!auth) return true;\n    const body = await readBodyJson(req);\n    const barcode = asString(body.barcode);\n    const name = asString(body.name);\n    if (!barcode || !name) {\n      json(res, 400, { error: \"barcode and name are required\" });\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    await upsertProduct({\n      siteId: auth.siteId,\n      barcode,\n      name,\n      category: asString(body.category),\n      unit: asString(body.unit),\n      registeredBy: auth.guardName,\n    });\n\n    // Seed a zero-quantity stock row so the new SKU appears on the\n    // Stock screen immediately, even before any receipts arrive.\n    await upsertStock({\n      siteId: auth.siteId,\n      barcode,\n      name,\n      category: asString(body.category),\n      unit: asString(body.unit),\n    });\n\n    json(res, 200, { barcode });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  if (pathname === \"/api/inventory/issues\" && method === \"POST\") {\n    const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n    if (!auth) return true;\n    const body = await readBodyJson(req);\n    const id = asString(body.id);\n    const barcode = asString(body.barcode);\n    const qty = asNumber(body.qty);\n    if (!id || !barcode || qty === null) {\n      json(res, 400, { error: \"id, barcode, qty are required\" });\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    const productName = asString(body.product_name) ?? \"Unknown\";\n    const result = await insertIssue({\n      id,\n      siteId: auth.siteId,\n      barcode,\n      productName,\n      qty,\n      reason: asString(body.reason),\n      takenBy: asString(body.taken_by),\n      deviceId: auth.deviceId,\n      guardId: auth.guardId,\n      issuedAt: isoOrNow(body.issued_at),\n    });\n\n    if (result.inserted) {\n      await adjustOnHand({\n        siteId: auth.siteId,\n        barcode,\n        delta: -qty,\n        fallbackName: productName,\n      });\n    }\n\n    json(res, 200, { id, inserted: result.inserted });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  if (pathname === \"/api/inventory/reorder-requests\" && method === \"POST\") {\n    const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n    if (!auth) return true;\n    const body = await readBodyJson(req);\n    const id = asString(body.id);\n    const barcode = asString(body.barcode);\n    if (!id || !barcode) {\n      json(res, 400, { error: \"id and barcode are required\" });\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    const result = await insertReorderRequest({\n      id,\n      siteId: auth.siteId,\n      barcode,\n      productName: asString(body.product_name) ?? \"Unknown\",\n      onHand: asNumber(body.on_hand),\n      threshold: asNumber(body.threshold),\n      requestedBy: auth.guardName,\n      deviceId: auth.deviceId,\n      guardId: auth.guardId,\n      requestedAt: isoOrNow(body.requested_at),\n    });\n\n    json(res, 200, { id, inserted: result.inserted });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  if (pathname === \"/api/inventory/mismatch-flags\" && method === \"POST\") {\n    const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n    if (!auth) return true;\n    const body = await readBodyJson(req);\n    const id = asString(body.id);\n    const barcode = asString(body.barcode);\n    if (!id || !barcode) {\n      json(res, 400, { error: \"id and barcode are required\" });\n      return true;\n    }\n\n    const result = await insertMismatchFlag({\n      id,\n      siteId: auth.siteId,\n      receiptId: asString(body.receipt_id),\n      orderItemId: asString(body.order_item_id),\n      barcode,\n      expected: asNumber(body.expected),\n      receivedTotal: asNumber(body.received_total),\n      note: asString(body.note),\n      deviceId: auth.deviceId,\n      guardId: auth.guardId,\n      flaggedAt: isoOrNow(body.flagged_at),\n    });\n\n    json(res, 200, { id, inserted: result.inserted });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n  if (pathname === \"/api/inventory/orders\" && method === \"GET\") {\n    const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n    if (!auth) return true;\n\n    const orders = await listOpenOrders(auth.siteId);\n    const items = await Promise.all(orders.map((o) => getOrderItems(o.id)));\n    const enriched = orders.map((o, i) => ({ ...o, items: items[i] }));\n    json(res, 200, { orders: enriched });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  if (pathname === \"/api/inventory/stock\" && method === \"GET\") {\n    const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n    if (!auth) return true;\n    json(res, 200, { stock: await listStock(auth.siteId) });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  if (pathname === \"/api/inventory/alerts\" && method === \"GET\") {\n    const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n    if (!auth) return true;\n    json(res, 200, { alerts: await listLowOrOut(auth.siteId) });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  if (pathname.startsWith(\"/api/inventory/catalog/\") && method === \"GET\") {\n    const auth = await authenticate(req, res);\n    if (!auth) return true;\n    const barcode = decodeURIComponent(pathname.replace(\"/api/inventory/catalog/\", \"\"));\n    const product = await findProduct(auth.siteId, barcode);\n    if (!product) {\n      // Look ahead at open orders so the field-app can pre-fill the\n      // receiving screen even for never-mapped barcodes.\n      const openItem = await findOpenItemByBarcode(auth.siteId, barcode);\n      json(res, 200, { product: null, openOrderItem: openItem });\n      return true;\n    }\n    const openItem = await findOpenItemByBarcode(auth.siteId, barcode);\n    json(res, 200, { product, openOrderItem: openItem });\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  return false;\n}\n"
}

id: toolu_01Vte75F49TkWakprAv5FTZ6
```
import * as Network from 'expo-network';
import { config } from '../config';
import { markFailed, markSending, markSent, nextBatch, pendingCount } from '../db/outbox';
import { api } from './api';

type Listener = (count: number) => void;

let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;
let running = false;
const listeners = new Set<Listener>();

export function onPendingChange(l: Listener): () => void {
  listeners.add(l);
  pendingCount().then(l).catch(() => {});
  return () => listeners.delete(l);
}

async function notify() {
  const c = await pendingCount().catch(() => 0);
  listeners.forEach((l) => l(c));
}

export async function flushOnce(): Promise<{ sent: number; failed: number }> {
  if (running) return { sent: 0, failed: 0 };
  running = true;
  let sent = 0;
  let failed = 0;
  try {
    const net = await Network.getNetworkStateAsync().catch(() => ({ isConnected: false }));
    if (!net.isConnected) return { sent, failed };

    const batch = await nextBatch(config.outboxBatchSize);
    for (const row of batch) {
      try {
        await markSending(row.id);
        const payload = JSON.parse(row.payload);
        if (row.kind === 'receipt') await api.postReceipt(payload);
        else if (row.kind === 'product_registration') await api.postProduct(payload);
        await markSent(row.id);
        sent++;
      } catch (err) {
        await markFailed(row.id, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
        failed++;
      }
    }
  } finally {
    running = false;
    await notify();
  }
  return { sent, failed };
}

export function startSync(): void {
  if (timer) return;
  flushOnce().catch(() => {});
  timer = setInterval(() => {
    flushOnce().catch(() => {});
  }, config.syncIntervalMs);
}

export function stopSync(): void {
  if (timer) clearInterval(timer);
  timer = null;
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/auth/session.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Session store for the guard's PIN-based login.\n *\n * Persists `{ token, guardId, guardName, language }` in AsyncStorage and\n * notifies subscribers when it changes. Network code (`sync/api.ts`)\n * reads the current token; the navigation root subscribes to know\n * whether to render the Login screen or the main stack.\n */\n\nimport AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';\n\nconst STORAGE_KEY = 'app.session';\n\nexport type GuardSession = {\n  token: string;\n  guardId: string;\n  guardName: string;\n  language: 'hindi' | 'english';\n};\n\ntype Listener = (session: GuardSession | null) => void;\n\nlet current: GuardSession | null = null;\nlet hydrated = false;\nconst listeners = new Set<Listener>();\n\nexport async function hydrateSession(): Promise<GuardSession | null> {\n  if (hydrated) return current;\n  hydrated = true;\n  try {\n    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n    if (raw) current = JSON.parse(raw) as GuardSession;\n  } catch {\n    current = null;\n  }\n  notify();\n  return current;\n}\n\nexport function getSession(): GuardSession | null {\n  return current;\n}\n\nexport async function setSession(s: GuardSession): Promise<void> {\n  current = s;\n  await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(s));\n  notify();\n}\n\nexport async function clearSession(): Promise<void> {\n  current = null;\n  await AsyncStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n  notify();\n}\n\nexport function onSessionChange(l: Listener): () => void {\n  listeners.add(l);\n  l(current);\n  return () => listeners.delete(l);\n}\n\nfunction notify() {\n  for (const l of listeners) l(current);\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/sync/api.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * Backend client for the office-ops inventory plugin.\n *\n * Every request:\n *   - is prefixed with `${config.apiBaseUrl}/api/inventory/...`\n *   - carries `x-guard-token` (from session) and `x-site-id` / `x-device-id`\n *   - throws an `ApiError` with status + body on non-2xx\n *\n * The outbox flusher (syncService) calls `api.send.*`; screens call\n * `api.fetch.*`. When no session token is present, requests still go out\n * — the server replies 401 and the outbox keeps the row queued (or\n * the read screens fall back to local cache).\n */\n\nimport { config } from '../config';\nimport { getSession } from '../auth/session';\nimport { getDeviceId } from '../utils/device';\n\nexport class ApiError extends Error {\n  constructor(public status: number, message: string, public body?: string) {\n    super(message);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function request<T>(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {\n  const deviceId = await getDeviceId();\n  const session = getSession();\n  const headers: Record<string, string> = {\n    'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n    'X-Device-Id': deviceId,\n    'X-Site-Id': config.siteId,\n    ...((init?.headers as Record<string, string>) ?? {}),\n  };\n  if (session?.token) headers['X-Guard-Token'] = session.token;\n\n  const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, { ...init, headers });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');\n    throw new ApiError(res.status, `HTTP ${res.status} on ${path}`, text.slice(0, 500));\n  }\n  return res.json() as Promise<T>;\n}\n\nconst post = (path: string, payload: unknown) =>\n  request<{ inserted?: boolean; id?: string; barcode?: string }>(path, {\n    method: 'POST',\n    body: JSON.stringify(payload),\n  });\n\nexport type RemoteOrderItem = {\n  id: string;\n  order_id: string;\n  barcode: string | null;\n  product_name: string;\n  expected_qty: number;\n  received_qty: number;\n};\n\nexport type RemoteOrder = {\n  id: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  vendor: string | null;\n  expected_at: string | null;\n  arrived_at: string | null;\n  status: string;\n  items: RemoteOrderItem[];\n};\n\nexport type RemoteStockRow = {\n  barcode: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string | null;\n  unit: string | null;\n  on_hand: number;\n  threshold: number;\n};\n\nexport type RemoteProduct = {\n  barcode: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string | null;\n  unit: string | null;\n};\n\nexport type RemoteCatalogHit = {\n  product: RemoteProduct | null;\n  openOrderItem: RemoteOrderItem | null;\n};\n\nexport const api = {\n  // ── Login is handled by visitor's guard auth endpoint, not the\n  //    inventory plugin. Lives here for convenience so screens have\n  //    one client to import.\n  async login(input: { guardName: string; pin: string }): Promise<{\n    token: string;\n    guardId: string;\n    guardName: string;\n    language: 'hindi' | 'english';\n  }> {\n    const deviceId = await getDeviceId();\n    const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}/api/guard/login`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        guardName: input.guardName,\n        pin: input.pin,\n        deviceId,\n        deviceName: 'field-app',\n      }),\n    });\n    if (!res.ok) {\n      const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');\n      throw new ApiError(res.status, `Login failed`, text.slice(0, 200));\n    }\n    return res.json();\n  },\n\n  // ── Writes (outbox-dispatched) ────────────────────────────\n  send: {\n    receipt: (payload: object) => post('/api/inventory/receipts', payload),\n    product: (payload: object) => post('/api/inventory/products', payload),\n    issue: (payload: object) => post('/api/inventory/issues', payload),\n    reorderRequest: (payload: object) =>\n      post('/api/inventory/reorder-requests', payload),\n    mismatchFlag: (payload: object) =>\n      post('/api/inventory/mismatch-flags', payload),\n  },\n\n  // ── Reads (called by screens) ─────────────────────────────\n  fetch: {\n    orders: () =>\n      request<{ orders: RemoteOrder[] }>('/api/inventory/orders').then((r) => r.orders),\n    stock: () =>\n      request<{ stock: RemoteStockRow[] }>('/api/inventory/stock').then((r) => r.stock),\n    alerts: () =>\n      request<{ alerts: RemoteStockRow[] }>('/api/inventory/alerts').then((r) => r.alerts),\n    catalog: (barcode: string) =>\n      request<RemoteCatalogHit>(\n        `/api/inventory/catalog/${encodeURIComponent(barcode)}`,\n      ),\n  },\n};\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/sync/syncService.ts",
  "content": "import * as Network from 'expo-network';\nimport { config } from '../config';\nimport { OutboxKind } from '../db/outbox';\nimport { markFailed, markSending, markSent, nextBatch, pendingCount } from '../db/outbox';\nimport { api } from './api';\n\ntype Listener = (count: number) => void;\n\nlet timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;\nlet running = false;\nconst listeners = new Set<Listener>();\n\nexport function onPendingChange(l: Listener): () => void {\n  listeners.add(l);\n  pendingCount().then(l).catch(() => {});\n  return () => listeners.delete(l);\n}\n\nasync function notify() {\n  const c = await pendingCount().catch(() => 0);\n  listeners.forEach((l) => l(c));\n}\n\n// Dispatch table for outbox kinds. Adding a new kind = one line here\n// + a handler in api.send.*.\nconst DISPATCH: Record<OutboxKind, (payload: object) => Promise<unknown>> = {\n  receipt: api.send.receipt,\n  product_registration: api.send.product,\n  issue: api.send.issue,\n  reorder_request: api.send.reorderRequest,\n  mismatch_flag: api.send.mismatchFlag,\n};\n\nexport async function flushOnce(): Promise<{ sent: number; failed: number }> {\n  if (running) return { sent: 0, failed: 0 };\n  running = true;\n  let sent = 0;\n  let failed = 0;\n  try {\n    const net = await Network.getNetworkStateAsync().catch(() => ({ isConnected: false }));\n    if (!net.isConnected) return { sent, failed };\n\n    const batch = await nextBatch(config.outboxBatchSize);\n    for (const row of batch) {\n      const dispatch = DISPATCH[row.kind];\n      if (!dispatch) {\n        await markFailed(row.id, `Unknown kind: ${row.kind}`);\n        failed++;\n        continue;\n      }\n      try {\n        await markSending(row.id);\n        await dispatch(JSON.parse(row.payload));\n        await markSent(row.id);\n        sent++;\n      } catch (err) {\n        await markFailed(row.id, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));\n        failed++;\n      }\n    }\n  } finally {\n    running = false;\n    await notify();\n  }\n  return { sent, failed };\n}\n\nexport function startSync(): void {\n  if (timer) return;\n  flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n  timer = setInterval(() => {\n    flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n  }, config.syncIntervalMs);\n}\n\nexport function stopSync(): void {\n  if (timer) clearInterval(timer);\n  timer = null;\n}\n"
}

{}),\n  };\n  if (session?.token) headers['X-Guard-Token'] = session.token;\n\n  const res = await fetch(`${config.apiBaseUrl}${path}`, { ...init, headers });\n  if (!res.ok) {\n    const text = await res.text().catch(() => '');\n    throw new ApiError(res.status, `HTTP ${res.status} on ${path}`, text.slice(0, 500));\n  }\n  return res.json() as Promise<T>;\n}\n\nconst post = (path: string, payload: unknown) =>\n  request<{ inserted?: boolean; id?: string; barcode?: string }>(path, {\n    method: 'POST',\n    body: JSON.stringify(payload),\n  });\n\nexport type RemoteOrderItem = {\n  id: string;\n  order_id: string;\n  barcode: string | null;\n  product_name: string;\n  expected_qty: number;\n  received_qty: number;\n};\n\nexport type RemoteOrder = {\n  id: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  vendor: string | null;\n  expected_at: string | null;\n  arrived_at: string | null;\n  status: string;\n  items: RemoteOrderItem[];\n};\n\nexport type RemoteStockRow = {\n  barcode: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string | null;\n  unit: string | null;\n  on_hand: number;\n  threshold: number;\n};\n\nexport type RemoteProduct = {\n  barcode: string;\n  site_id: string;\n  name: string;\n  category: string | null;\n  unit: string | null;\n};\n\nexport type RemoteCatalogHit = {\n  product: RemoteProduct | null;\n  openOrderItem: RemoteOrderItem | null;\n};\n\nexport const api = {\n  // Login is handled by visitor's guard auth endpoint, not the inventory\n  // plugin.   "content": "import * as Network from 'expo-network';\nimport { config } from '../config';\nimport { OutboxKind } from '../db/outbox';\nimport { markFailed, markSending, markSent, nextBatch, pendingCount } from '../db/outbox';\nimport { api } from './api';\n\ntype Listener = (count: number) => void;\n\nlet timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | null = null;\nlet running = false;\nconst listeners = new Set<Listener>();\n\nexport function onPendingChange(l: Listener): () => void {\n  listeners.add(l);\n  pendingCount().then(l).catch(() => {});\n  return () => listeners.delete(l);\n}\n\nasync function notify() {\n  const c = await pendingCount().catch(() => 0);\n  listeners.forEach((l) => l(c));\n}\n\n// Dispatch table for outbox kinds. Adding a new kind = one line here\n// + a handler in api.send.*.\nconst DISPATCH: Record<OutboxKind, (payload: object) => Promise<unknown>> = {\n  receipt: api.send.receipt,\n  product_registration: api.send.product,\n  issue: api.send.issue,\n  reorder_request: api.send.reorderRequest,\n  mismatch_flag: api.send.mismatchFlag,\n};\n\nexport async function flushOnce(): Promise<{ sent: number; failed: number }> {\n  if (running) return { sent: 0, failed: 0 };\n  running = true;\n  let sent = 0;\n  let failed = 0;\n  try {\n    const net = await Network.getNetworkStateAsync().catch(() => ({ isConnected: false }));\n    if (!net.isConnected) return { sent, failed };\n\n    const batch = await nextBatch(config.outboxBatchSize);\n    for (const row of batch) {\n      const dispatch = DISPATCH[row.kind];\n      if (!dispatch) {\n        await markFailed(row.id, `Unknown kind: ${row.kind}`);\n        failed++;\n        continue;\n      }\n      try {\n        await markSending(row.id);\n        await dispatch(JSON.parse(row.payload));\n        await markSent(row.id);\n        sent++;\n      } catch (err) {\n        await markFailed(row.id, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));\n        failed++;\n      }\n    }\n  } finally {\n    running = false;\n    await notify();\n  }\n  return { sent, failed };\n}\n\nexport function startSync(): void {\n  if (timer) return;\n  flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n  timer = setInterval(() => {\n    flushOnce().catch(() => {});\n  }, config.syncIntervalMs);\n}\n\nexport function stopSync(): void {\n  if (timer) clearInterval(timer);\n  timer = null;\n}\n"
}

1	/**
2	 * RBAC stub. Phase 1: there is no auth yet, so `useCan()` returns true for
3	 * everything. The shape is real, though — modules can already declare the
4	 * permissions they require, and screens can call `useCan()` to gate UI.
5	 *
6	 * When auth lands, replace the implementation with a real check against the
7	 * authenticated user's role/permission set. The contract here doesn't change.
8	 */
9	
10	import { useCallback } from 'react';
11	
12	// Permissions are namespaced strings: `<module>.<verb>` or `<module>.<resource>.<verb>`.
13	// Keep them short; resolve them against a real policy engine later.
14	export type Permission = string;
15	
16	export type Role = 'guard' | 'supervisor' | 'manager' | 'admin';
17	
18	export type Principal = {
19	  userId: string;
20	  roles: Role[];
21	  permissions: Permission[];
22	};
23	
24	// Placeholder principal. Replace with the real signed-in user once auth lands.
25	export const ANONYMOUS_PRINCIPAL: Principal = {
26	  userId: 'anon',
27	  roles: ['guard'],
28	  permissions: ['*'],
29	};
30	
31	export function can(principal: Principal, permission: Permission): boolean {
32	  if (principal.permissions.includes('*')) return true;
33	  return principal.permissions.includes(permission);
34	}
35	
36	/**
37	 * Hook form for use in components. Returns a stable checker so callers can
38	 * do `const can = useCan(); can('inventory.receive')`.
39	 */
40	export function useCan() {
41	  return useCallback((permission: Permission) => can(ANONYMOUS_PRINCIPAL, permission), []);
42	}
43	
44	export function useHasPermission(permission: Permission): boolean {
45	  return useCan()(permission);
46	}
47	
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx",
  "content": "import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { ANONYMOUS_PRINCIPAL, can, Principal } from '../rbac';\nimport { api } from '../sync/api';\nimport { clearSession, GuardSession, getSession, hydrateSession, onSessionChange, setSession } from './session';\n\ntype Ctx = {\n  /** True once we've read AsyncStorage for any persisted session. */\n  ready: boolean;\n  session: GuardSession | null;\n  principal: Principal;\n  login: (input: { guardName: string; pin: string }) => Promise<void>;\n  logout: () => Promise<void>;\n};\n\nconst AuthContext = createContext<Ctx | null>(null);\n\nfunction principalFromSession(session: GuardSession | null): Principal {\n  if (!session) return ANONYMOUS_PRINCIPAL;\n  // Backend doesn't yet return roles/permissions for the guard. Until it\n  // does, every logged-in guard gets the inventory module's view +\n  // capture permissions. Replace with server-supplied values when the\n  // /api/guard/login response grows them.\n  return {\n    userId: session.guardId,\n    roles: ['guard'],\n    permissions: [\n      'inventory.view',\n      'inventory.receive',\n      'inventory.issue',\n      'inventory.register',\n      'inventory.reorder',\n    ],\n  };\n}\n\nexport function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  const [session, setSessionState] = useState<GuardSession | null>(getSession());\n  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    hydrateSession().then((s) => {\n      setSessionState(s);\n      setReady(true);\n    });\n    return onSessionChange(setSessionState);\n  }, []);\n\n  const login = useCallback(async ({ guardName, pin }: { guardName: string; pin: string }) => {\n    const res = await api.login({ guardName, pin });\n    await setSession({\n      token: res.token,\n      guardId: res.guardId,\n      guardName: res.guardName,\n      language: res.language,\n    });\n  }, []);\n\n  const logout = useCallback(async () => {\n    await clearSession();\n  }, []);\n\n  return (\n    <AuthContext.Provider\n      value={{\n        ready,\n        session,\n        principal: principalFromSession(session),\n        login,\n        logout,\n      }}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </AuthContext.Provider>\n  );\n}\n\nexport function useAuth(): Ctx {\n  const ctx = useContext(AuthContext);\n  if (!ctx) throw new Error('useAuth must be inside AuthProvider');\n  return ctx;\n}\n\n/**\n * Permission checker tied to the live session. Replaces the stub in\n * `rbac/permissions.ts` for use inside the React tree.\n */\nexport function useCan() {\n  const { principal } = useAuth();\n  return useCallback((permission: string) => can(principal, permission), [principal]);\n}\n"
}

  "content": "import React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport {\n  KeyboardAvoidingView,\n  Platform,\n  ScrollView,\n  StyleSheet,\n  View,\n} from 'react-native';\nimport { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';\nimport { useAuth } from '../../../auth';\nimport {\n  Button,\n  palette,\n  spacing,\n  Text,\n  TextField,\n} from '../../../design';\nimport { useT } from '../../../i18n';\nimport { haptic } from '../../../utils/haptics';\n\nexport function LoginScreen() {\n  const t = useT();\n  const { login } = useAuth();\n  const [guardName, setGuardName] = useState('');\n  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');\n  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  const canSubmit = guardName.trim().length > 0 && pin.trim().length >= 4 && !busy;\n\n  const submit = async () => {\n    if (!canSubmit) return;\n    setBusy(true);\n    setError(null);\n    try {\n      await login({ guardName: guardName.trim(), pin: pin.trim() });\n      haptic.success();\n      // AuthProvider flips state → RootNavigator swaps to the main stack.\n    } catch (e) {\n      haptic.warn();\n      setError(e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e));\n    } finally {\n      setBusy(false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <SafeAreaView edges={['top', 'bottom']} style={styles.safe}>\n      <KeyboardAvoidingView\n        behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}\n        style={{ flex: 1 }}\n      >\n        <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={styles.scroll} keyboardShouldPersistTaps=\"handled\">\n          <View style={styles.header}>\n            <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              {t('guard').toUpperCase()}\n            </Text>\n            <Text variant=\"displayMedium\" style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}>\n              {t('appName')}\n            </Text>\n            <Text\n              variant=\"bodyLarge\"\n              color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n              style={{ marginTop: spacing.md }}\n            >\n              Sign in with your name and PIN.\n            </Text>\n          </View>\n\n          <View style={{ marginTop: spacing.xl, gap: spacing.lg }}>\n            <TextField\n              label=\"Guard name\"\n              value={guardName}\n              onChangeText={setGuardName}\n              placeholder=\"As registered by your supervisor\"\n              autoCapitalize=\"words\"\n              returnKeyType=\"next\"\n            />\n            <TextField\n              label=\"PIN\"\n              value={pin}\n              onChangeText={setPin}\n              placeholder=\"4–6 digits\"\n              keyboardType=\"number-pad\"\n              secureTextEntry\n              maxLength={6}\n              returnKeyType=\"done\"\n              onSubmitEditing={submit}\n            />\n            {error ? (\n              <Text variant=\"bodyMedium\" color={palette.error}>\n                {error}\n              </Text>\n            ) : null}\n          </View>\n        </ScrollView>\n\n        <View style={styles.footer}>\n          <Button\n            label={busy ? 'Signing in…' : 'Sign in'}\n            onPress={submit}\n            disabled={!canSubmit}\n            loading={busy}\n            size=\"lg\"\n            fullWidth\n          />\n        </View>\n      </KeyboardAvoidingView>\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  safe: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: palette.background },\n  scroll: { padding: spacing.xl, flexGrow: 1 },\n  header: { marginTop: spacing.xxl },\n  footer: {\n    padding: spacing.xl,\n    borderTopWidth: 1,\n    borderTopColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n    backgroundColor: palette.surface,\n  },\n});\n"
}

(\n          <Stack.Screen name=\"Login\" component={LoginScreen} options={{ animation: 'fade' }} />\n        ) : (\n          screens.map((s) => (\n            <Stack.Screen\n              key={s.name}\n              name={s.name}\n              component={s.component}\n              options={s.options as any}\n            />\n          ))\n        )}\n      </Stack.Navigator>\n    </NavigationContainer>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/rbac/permissions.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * RBAC primitives: types + pure permission checker.\n *\n * The hook form (`useCan`) lives in `src/auth/AuthProvider.tsx` because\n * it depends on the live session. This file stays React-free so it can\n * be used from non-component code (db helpers, sync workers, tests).\n */\n\n// Permissions are namespaced strings: `<module>.<verb>` or\n// `<module>.<resource>.<verb>`. Keep them short; resolve against a real\n// policy engine later.\nexport type Permission = string;\n\nexport type Role = 'guard' | 'supervisor' | 'manager' | 'admin';\n\nexport type Principal = {\n  userId: string;\n  roles: Role[];\n  permissions: Permission[];\n};\n\n// Stand-in for the not-yet-authenticated user. Holds zero permissions\n// so any gated UI hides until the real session loads.\nexport const ANONYMOUS_PRINCIPAL: Principal = {\n  userId: 'anon',\n  roles: [],\n  permissions: [],\n};\n\nexport function can(principal: Principal, permission: Permission): boolean {\n  if (principal.permissions.includes('*')) return true;\n  return principal.permissions.includes(permission);\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/design/Card.tsx",
  "content": "import React from 'react';\nimport { Pressable, StyleProp, View, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';\nimport { useTheme } from '../theme';\nimport { radius, spacing } from './tokens';\n\ntype Props = {\n  onPress?: () => void;\n  tone?: 'filled' | 'outlined' | 'elevated';\n  padding?: keyof typeof spacing;\n  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;\n  children: React.ReactNode;\n};\n\nexport function Card({ onPress, tone = 'filled', padding = 'lg', style, children }: Props) {\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const Container: any = onPress ? Pressable : View;\n  const bg =\n    tone === 'outlined'\n      ? palette.surface\n      : tone === 'elevated'\n      ? palette.surfaceContainerLowest\n      : palette.surfaceContainerLow;\n\n  return (\n    <Container\n      onPress={onPress}\n      android_ripple={onPress ? { color: palette.outlineVariant } : undefined}\n      style={[\n        {\n          backgroundColor: bg,\n          borderRadius: radius.lg,\n          padding: spacing[padding],\n          borderWidth: tone === 'outlined' ? 1 : 0,\n          borderColor: palette.outlineVariant,\n          ...(tone === 'elevated'\n            ? {\n                shadowColor: '#000',\n                shadowOpacity: 0.08,\n                shadowRadius: 6,\n                shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 2 },\n                elevation: 2,\n              }\n            : null),\n          overflow: 'hidden',\n        },\n        style,\n      ]}\n    >\n      {children}\n    </Container>\n  );\n}\n"
}

(\n            <Text variant=\"labelMedium\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant}>\n              {subtitle}\n            </Text>\n          ) : null}\n        </View>\n        <View style={styles.trailing}>{trailing}</View>\n      </View>\n    </SafeAreaView>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  row: {\n    minHeight: 56,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm,\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n  },\n  leading: { width: 56, alignItems: 'flex-start' },\n  trailing: { minWidth: 56, alignItems: 'flex-end', paddingRight: spacing.sm },\n  titleWrap: { flex: 1, paddingHorizontal: spacing.sm },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/design/IconButton.tsx",
  "content": "import React from 'react';\nimport { Pressable, StyleProp, StyleSheet, ViewStyle } from 'react-native';\nimport { useTheme } from '../theme';\nimport { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';\nimport { Text } from './Text';\nimport { hitSlop, radius } from './tokens';\n\ntype Props = {\n  icon: string;\n  onPress: () => void;\n  size?: number;\n  tone?: 'default' | 'inverse';\n  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;\n};\n\nexport function IconButton({ icon, onPress, size = 44, tone = 'default', style }: Props) {\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const color = tone === 'inverse' ? palette.surface : palette.onSurface;\n  return (\n    <Pressable\n      hitSlop={hitSlop}\n      onPress={() => {\n        haptic.tap();\n        onPress();\n      }}\n      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant, borderless: true, radius: size / 2 }}\n      style={[styles.base, { width: size, height: size, borderRadius: radius.pill }, style]}\n    >\n      <Text variant=\"titleLarge\" color={color}>\n        {icon}\n      </Text>\n    </Pressable>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  base: { alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center' },\n});\n"
}

{

  "content": "import React from 'react';\nimport { Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { useTheme } from '../theme';\nimport { haptic } from '../utils/haptics';\nimport { Text } from './Text';\nimport { radius, spacing } from './tokens';\n\ntype Props = {\n  label: string;\n  selected?: boolean;\n  onPress: () => void;\n  leadingIcon?: string;\n};\n\nexport function Chip({ label, selected, onPress, leadingIcon }: Props) {\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  return (\n    <Pressable\n      onPress={() => {\n        haptic.tap();\n        onPress();\n      }}\n      android_ripple={{ color: palette.outlineVariant }}\n      style={[\n        styles.base,\n        {\n          backgroundColor: selected ? palette.secondaryContainer : palette.surface,\n          borderColor: selected ? palette.secondaryContainer : palette.outlineVariant,\n        },\n      ]}\n    >\n      <View style={styles.row}>\n        {selected ? (\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSecondaryContainer}>\n            ✓\n          </Text>\n        ) : leadingIcon ? (\n          <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurface}>\n            {leadingIcon}\n          </Text>\n        ) : null}\n        <Text\n          variant=\"labelLarge\"\n          color={selected ? palette.onSecondaryContainer : palette.onSurface}\n        >\n          {label}\n        </Text>\n      </View>\n    </Pressable>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  base: {\n    minHeight: 40,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,\n    borderRadius: radius.sm,\n    borderWidth: 1,\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    justifyContent: 'center',\n    overflow: 'hidden',\n  },\n  row: { flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: spacing.sm },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/design/StatusPill.tsx",
  "content": "import React from 'react';\nimport { StyleSheet, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { useTheme } from '../theme';\nimport { Text } from './Text';\nimport { radius, spacing } from './tokens';\n\ntype Tone = 'success' | 'warn' | 'danger' | 'neutral';\n\ntype Props = {\n  label: string;\n  tone?: Tone;\n  leadingIcon?: string;\n};\n\nexport function StatusPill({ label, tone = 'neutral', leadingIcon }: Props) {\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const toneMap: Record<Tone, { bg: string; fg: string }> = {\n    success: { bg: palette.primaryContainer, fg: palette.onPrimaryContainer },\n    warn: { bg: palette.warnContainer, fg: palette.onWarnContainer },\n    danger: { bg: palette.errorContainer, fg: palette.onErrorContainer },\n    neutral: { bg: palette.surfaceContainerHigh, fg: palette.onSurfaceVariant },\n  };\n  const t = toneMap[tone];\n  return (\n    <View style={[styles.base, { backgroundColor: t.bg }]}>\n      {leadingIcon ? (\n        <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={t.fg}>\n          {leadingIcon}\n        </Text>\n      ) : null}\n      <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={t.fg}>\n        {label}\n      </Text>\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  base: {\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.md,\n    paddingVertical: spacing.xs,\n    borderRadius: radius.pill,\n    alignSelf: 'flex-start',\n    flexDirection: 'row',\n    alignItems: 'center',\n    gap: spacing.xs,\n  },\n});\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/field-app/src/design/TextField.tsx",
  "content": "import React, { useState } from 'react';\nimport { StyleSheet, TextInput, TextInputProps, View } from 'react-native';\nimport { useTheme } from '../theme';\nimport { Text } from './Text';\nimport { radius, spacing, type as typo } from './tokens';\n\ntype Props = TextInputProps & {\n  label: string;\n  helper?: string;\n  error?: string;\n};\n\nexport function TextField({ label, helper, error, onFocus, onBlur, style, ...rest }: Props) {\n  const { palette } = useTheme();\n  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false);\n  const borderColor = error\n    ? palette.error\n    : focused\n    ? palette.primary\n    : palette.outlineVariant;\n  return (\n    <View>\n      <Text variant=\"labelLarge\" color={palette.onSurfaceVariant} style={{ marginBottom: spacing.xs }}>\n        {label}\n      </Text>\n      <TextInput\n        {...rest}\n        onFocus={(e) => {\n          setFocused(true);\n          onFocus?.(e);\n        }}\n        onBlur={(e) => {\n          setFocused(false);\n          onBlur?.(e);\n        }}\n        placeholderTextColor={palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n        style={[\n          styles.input,\n          typo.bodyLarge,\n          {\n            backgroundColor: palette.surfaceContainerLowest,\n            borderColor,\n            color: palette.onSurface,\n            borderWidth: focused ? 2 : 1.5,\n          },\n          style,\n        ]}\n      />\n      {(error || helper) && (\n        <Text\n          variant=\"labelMedium\"\n          color={error ? palette.error : palette.onSurfaceVariant}\n          style={{ marginTop: spacing.xs }}\n        >\n          {error ?? helper}\n        </Text>\n      )}\n    </View>\n  );\n}\n\nconst styles = StyleSheet.create({\n  input: {\n    borderRadius: radius.md,\n    paddingHorizontal: spacing.lg,\n    minHeight: 56,\n  },\n});\n"
}
